### [1] [DTM: Deterministic Approaches for Black-box Test Suite Minimization with Tree-based Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04205)
*Md Siam,Shartaz Sajid Nahid,Md Arif Hasan,Nurul Ahad Tawhid,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DTM，一种面向黑盒测试套件最小化的确定性框架，通过AST相似度分析与确定性子集选择算法，在保持测试效果的同时降低测试成本并消除运行间不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效的黑盒测试套件最小化方法主要依赖进化搜索算法，具有非确定性，导致不同运行结果不一致，削弱自动化测试流水线的可靠性。因此，需要一种兼具有效性、效率与完全确定性的解决方案。

Method: DTM首先将测试用例转换为抽象语法树，并采用四种基于树的度量计算测试用例之间的两两相似度；随后使用改进的最小生成树、谱聚类和动态规划三种确定性算法进行测试子集选择。实验基于Defects4J中的16个Java项目和661个缺陷版本展开。

Result: DTM的平均准确率达到0.74，平均执行时间仅为0.98分钟，优于所有对比的先进方法。此外，多次运行均生成完全相同的结果，验证了其完全确定性。

Conclusion: DTM能够在不访问生产代码的条件下，实现高效、有效且可复现的黑盒测试套件最小化，为对结果稳定性要求较高的自动化测试流水线提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Black-box Test Suite Minimization (TSM) techniques reduce testing costs without requiring access to production code. However, existing effective approaches rely on evolutionary search algorithms, introducing non-determinism that produces inconsistent results across runs, undermining reliability in automated testing pipelines. We propose DTM (Deterministic approaches for black-box Test suite Minimization), a framework that ensures deterministic test suite reduction while preserving effectiveness and efficiency. DTM converts test cases into Abstract Syntax Trees and computes pairwise similarities using four tree-based measures. For subset selection, it employs three deterministic algorithms: Modified Minimum Spanning Tree, Spectral Clustering, and Dynamic Programming. We evaluated DTM on 16 Java projects from Defects4J with 661 buggy versions. Experimental results show that DTM achieved an average accuracy of 0.74 with an execution time of just 0.98 minutes, outperforming all state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, it consistently produced identical results across multiple runs, ensuring full determinism.

</details>


### [2] [Breaking the Alphabet: Rethinking File Ordering in Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04207)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Zadia Codabux,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了1,355名专业开发者，发现Pull Request中的默认字母排序通常不符合代码审查者的认知与推理方式，可能增加上下文切换、审查疲劳及漏检缺陷的风险。研究支持采用依赖关系感知分组和可自定义的文件排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码审查工具普遍采用字母排序，虽然具有可预测性，但未必符合多文件代码变更的逻辑结构。既有研究关注文件位置对注意力的影响，却较少解释排序方式如何影响认知负荷、理解过程和审查完整性，因此有必要从开发者体验与审查效果角度开展大规模研究。

Method: 研究对来自182个常用开源项目的1,355名专业开发者开展大规模调查，并结合定量与定性方法分析文件排序对审查行为、代码理解和感知有效性的影响。同时，研究识别多文件审查中的主要困难，并收集开发者对改进审查工具的需求。

Result: 仅10.2%的受访者认为字母排序是最佳方案。尽管部分开发者认可其可预测性，但57.6%的受访者表示该排序会增加上下文切换、打断逻辑推理并加剧审查疲劳；63.9%担心默认排序可能导致漏发现缺陷。另有66%的受访者希望使用可自定义的文件排序，依赖关系感知分组也被认为是重要改进方向。

Conclusion: 文件排序并非单纯的界面呈现问题，而会影响代码审查中的认知负荷、逻辑理解和缺陷发现。代码审查工具应从固定的字母排序转向以审查者为中心的设计，提供基于依赖关系、变更逻辑和用户偏好的灵活排序机制，以提升审查效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Effective code review is central to maintaining software quality, yet there is limited research about how the ordering of changed files in Pull Requests (PRs) influences review effectiveness. Most popular code review tools default to alphabetical ordering, favoring predictability over contextual relevance. While prior studies examined how file position shapes reviewer attention, it remains unclear how such ordering influences cognitive load and perceived review thoroughness. This study presents the first large-scale survey of 1,355 professional developers across 182 widely used open-source projects to investigate how file ordering impacts review behavior, comprehension, and perceived effectiveness. Our mixed-methods analysis reveals that only 10.2% of reviewers consider alphabetical ordering optimal, underscoring a cognitive misalignment in their interpretation of code changes. Although some developers appreciate its predictability, more than half (57.6%) report that it increases context switching, disrupts logical reasoning, and contributes to review fatigue, and 63.9% expressed concern that the default ordering may cause them to miss bugs. We further identify key challenges in multi-file reviews and elicit developers' expectations for improved tooling, including dependency-aware grouping and customizable file ordering (requested by 66% of reviewers). These findings highlight the need for reviewer-centric interface designs that better align tool behavior with human cognition.

</details>


### [3] [AI Writes Code, Humans Pay the Debt. An Empirical Study on the Sustainability and Evolution of Agent-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04208)
*Antonino Coppola,Matteo Esposito,Rick Kazman,Valentina Lenarduzzi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究拟通过大规模软件仓库挖掘，比较生成式AI编码代理与人类开发者提交的修复代码，评估其在问题定位、技术债引入及长期软件演化方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码代理已带来短期开发效率提升，但其生成代码是否会降低软件质量、增加技术债，以及这些影响是否会在后续版本中累积，尚缺乏系统性的实证证据。

Method: 研究计划基于SQuaD数据集，从约62.8万个问题工单构建候选集合；针对工单生成由多个经系统基准评测筛选的LLM代理完成的实现，并与开发者实际提交进行对照。评估将结合静态分析指标与工具，在提交级别及跨发布版本级别分析问题定位准确性、技术债变化和演化趋势。

Result: 摘要尚未报告实测结果，仅提出预期：AI代理代码可能在定位准确率、技术债引入程度和长期演化轨迹上与人工提交存在差异；不同LLM代理之间也预计具有显著变异性。

Conclusion: 该研究的潜在贡献在于建立面向真实仓库和长期演化视角的AI代理代码质量评估证据，为理解代理辅助开发的质量代价与收益、选择合适模型及制定更负责任的工程采用策略提供依据。

Abstract: Context. The increasing adoption of Generative AI coding agents in software engineering is transforming how developers implement and maintain code. While these systems provide short-term productivity benefits, their long-term impact on software quality and technical debt remains unclear. Aim. We aim to investigate how agent-generated code affects software quality, focusing on issue localization accuracy, the introduction of technical debt, and its evolution over time. Method. We will conduct a large-scale mining software repositories study using the SQuaD dataset, employing a candidate set of 628k issue tickets. We will generate agent-based implementations for these issues, and compare them with the actual commits done by developers using static analysis metrics and tools. We will analyze differences at the commit level and across releases, and we will consider multiple LLM-based Agents selected through a systematic benchmarking strategy. Expected Results. We expect to provide empirical evidence on the trade-offs introduced by agent-based development, including differences in localization accuracy, variations in technical debt introduction, and potential divergence in long-term evolution. We expect the results to highlight variability across LLMs, to enrich our understanding of software evolution with Agents, and to inform more responsible adoption of Agents in software development.

</details>


### [4] [The Prompt Triangle: A Registered Report on Prompts as Hybrid Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04209)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一项预注册的确认性研究协议，旨在将用于代码生成的提示词视为软件工程工件，检验提示词的演化特征及其与需求工程活动的一致性是否能够预测开发成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编程助手使开发者的工作重心部分由直接编写代码转向设计和迭代提示词，但提示词如何承载需求意图、架构指导与实现约束，以及这些特征如何影响开发结果，尚缺乏充分的实证证据。

Method: 研究以 Prompt Triangle 为理论框架，将提示词划分为功能与质量、通用解决方案、特定解决方案三个组成部分。作者预注册四项假设，围绕提示词演化、开发者特征、与需求工程活动的对齐程度及其时间模式展开检验；数据来源包括30人的受控实验、社区上传样本和挖掘数据，并采用双人编码提高标注一致性。

Result: 摘要仅报告 Stage 1 研究方案，未提供假设检验、效应量或统计显著性等实证结果。其预期产出是识别能够预测开发成功的提示模式，并刻画提示词在开发过程中的变化规律。

Conclusion: 该研究将提示词系统化为兼具需求表达与解决方案指导属性的工程工件，并通过预注册、多来源数据和双人编码开展确认性验证。其贡献取决于后续对提示词编码有效性、开发成功指标定义、样本代表性及不同数据源可比性的实证检验。

Abstract: AI-based coding assistants are transforming software development by shifting effort from writing code to crafting prompts that guide code generation. Despite their growing importance, little empirical evidence exists on how prompts function as software engineering artifacts. Building on prior work framing prompts as mixed artifacts combining requirement intent and solution guidance, we build on the Prompt Triangle, a conceptual model describing prompts along three components: Functionality and Quality (requirements), General Solutions (architectural guidance), and Specific Solutions (implementation constraints). This registered report presents the Stage 1 protocol for a confirmatory study investigating how prompts evolve and how their alignment with requirements engineering (RE) activities predicts development outcomes. We preregister four hypotheses examining prompt evolution, developer characteristics, RE activity alignment, and temporal patterns. We employ a controlled experiment (n=30), community uploads, and mined data using dual-coding to test whether prompting patterns predict development success.

</details>


### [5] [SH-PDOPS: AI-Driven Cloud Native Enterprise Reliability Framework for Predictive Analytics and Intelligent DevOps Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04210)
*Ayushman Bosu Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种人工智能驱动的云原生企业可靠性框架，融合机器学习、Kubernetes、可观测性与自动化事件响应，以提升分布式基础设施的预测分析、运维自动化和系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业分布式基础设施复杂度不断提升，传统可靠性工程难以及时预测故障、定位异常并快速恢复，因此需要将人工智能与云原生DevOps相结合，实现更主动、自适应和自动化的基础设施管理。

Method: 框架集成机器学习模型、Kubernetes编排、可观测性数据管道、自动化事件响应和CI/CD流程，重点研究故障预测检测、异常监控、自愈基础设施及持续交付优化，并通过实验评估其运维效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够提升运维效率，减少系统停机时间，并增强企业基础设施的可扩展性；同时支持更高效的故障预测、异常处理和自动化运维。

Conclusion: 将人工智能融入云原生DevOps能够构建更具适应性和自治能力的企业可靠性体系。该框架为实现预测性运维、自愈基础设施和弹性管理提供了具有实践价值的方法，但摘要未说明具体数据集、基线方法和量化改进幅度。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven cloud-native enterprise reliability framework designed to improve predictive analytics, intelligent DevOps automation, and system resilience in modern distributed infrastructures. The proposed framework integrates machine learning models, Kubernetes orchestration, observability pipelines, and automated incident response mechanisms to enhance reliability engineering practices. The study explores predictive failure detection, anomaly monitoring, self-healing infrastructure, and CI/CD optimization using cloud-native technologies. Experimental evaluation demonstrates improved operational efficiency, reduced downtime, and enhanced scalability for enterprise environments. The framework provides a practical approach for combining artificial intelligence with DevOps methodologies to achieve adaptive and autonomous infrastructure management.

</details>


### [6] [Data-Related Challenges and Requirements for Event Log Generation in Process Mining: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04211)
*Ghita El Alaoui Talibi,Oleksandr Kosenkov,Anastasija Nikiforova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了流程挖掘中事件日志生成阶段的数据相关挑战、需求与解决方案，指出数据质量是当前关键问题，并强调数据工程与需求工程的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流程挖掘研究较多关注数据质量，却对作为流程挖掘起点的事件日志生成及其与数据工程、需求工程之间的关系关注不足，因此有必要系统梳理相关问题与实践。

Method: 开展系统性文献综述，分析事件日志生成中的数据相关挑战、需求类型及已有解决方案，并归纳其与数据工程和领域知识工程的联系。

Result: 研究识别出29项挑战，并将其归纳为5类基础需求；现有事件日志生成方案已不同程度地融入数据工程技术和领域知识工程技术。

Conclusion: 事件日志生成是影响流程挖掘结果的重要基础环节。研究成果可帮助研究者和实践者把握相关趋势，并利用数据工程与需求工程方法提升日志生成质量，进而改善流程挖掘效果。

Abstract: Process mining is becoming an essential technology that not only supports the improvement of business processes but also enables the software technologies underlying the future digital economy. Among the multiple challenges discussed by the process mining community, data quality is emerging as the most pressing one. However, insufficient attention has been paid to event log generation as the very first phase of process mining and to its relationship with data engineering and requirements engineering. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic literature review of data-related challenges, requirements, and solutions in event log generation. Our results show that a wide range of 29 challenges can be addressed with 5 basic categories of requirements. Existing solutions already embed some form of data engineering and domain knowledge engineering techniques for event log generation. This review can support researchers and practitioners in understanding current trends in event log generation for process mining and in leveraging data and requirements engineering techniques to improve log generation and, ultimately, process mining outcomes.

</details>


### [7] [Big Questions on Software Architecture: Report of the ICSE 2026 BoF on Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04212)
*Davide Taibi,Patricia Lago,Henry Muccini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发起并总结了一项软件架构研究社区倡议，旨在识别真正值得科学研究的重大问题，并区分它们与技术趋势或短期热点。当前仅报告了在ICSE会议第一场讨论中的初步结果，后续将结合ICSA会议第二场讨论继续完善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构对技术、社会、环境以及人机关系的影响日益广泛。作者认为，尽管该领域重要性不断提升，研究社区仍需反思哪些问题具有长期科学价值，哪些只是技术潮流或研究热点。

Method: 组织社区讨论活动，在2026年的ICSE和ICSA会议中分别举办两场专题讨论，收集参与者对软件架构重大研究问题的观点，并对讨论结果进行整理。本文仅分析第一场会议的结果，并计划纳入第二场会议的数据。

Result: 论文报告了第一场社区讨论的初步成果，但摘要没有给出具体的重大问题清单、参与人数、数据分析流程或定量结论。研究仍处于阶段性汇报状态，完整结果有待后续会议讨论补充。

Conclusion: 本文是一项面向研究议程构建的社区驱动型倡议，核心贡献在于推动软件架构领域识别长期、基础性和高影响力的科学问题。由于摘要以“To be continued”结尾，当前结论尚不完整，最终研究议程和问题分类仍需等待后续结果。

Abstract: Software Architecture (SA) is more important than ever, from the need to master the pervasive impact of technology in society and planet earth, to balancing the role of humans versus technology, flexibility versus chaos, economic convenience versus digital autonomy. Despite SA importance, we feel that the SA research community needs to reflect on what are the big questions that deserve scientific research, and what are instead technology trends or hypes. In this paper, we report the results of a community initiative aiming to identify and discuss the big questions in SA research. Organised in two sessions hosted in 2026 at the ICSE and ICSA conferences, respectively, this paper reports on the results collected during the first session, and looks forward to complementing them with the results of the second one. To be continued ...

</details>


### [8] [Toward Model-Driven Digital Twin Configuration: Separating Structure Semantics and Runtime with SysML SAREF and Ditto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04213)
*Andrey Sadovykh,Matthew Rusakov,Kirill Korikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向能源社区数字孪生的分层建模方法：使用SysML描述系统结构，使用SAREF4ENER提供能源领域语义，并通过W3C Web of Things Thing Description将模型映射至Eclipse Ditto运行平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生配置主要依赖人工编写，字段命名任意、单位缺乏明确表达，且设备关系没有机器可读描述，导致配置一致性、可追溯性和跨系统互操作能力不足。

Method: 提出三个互补工具的职责划分：SysML负责系统结构建模，SAREF4ENER负责概念和语义标准化，Eclipse Ditto负责运行时数字孪生表示。论文给出从带有SAREF4ENER标注的SysML模型推导Ditto配置的概念规则，并以Modelio建立的温室能源社区节点模型进行示例说明。该方法目前属于设计时规则，尚未实现为可执行生成器。

Result: 案例分析表明，该分层方案能够增强模型到数字孪生配置之间的可追溯性和配置一致性，并为设备、属性及关系提供语义互操作所需的机器可读接口。相较于纯手工配置，它具有更清晰的职责分工和标准化基础。

Conclusion: 论文主张将结构建模、领域语义和运行时数字孪生平台分离并协同使用，是提升能源社区数字孪生可维护性与互操作性的可行方向。贡献主要限于设计时配置，尚未解决自动生成、运行时同步及完整验证等问题。

Abstract: Energy communities, neighbourhoods that jointly produce, store, and consume electricity, increasingly rely on \emph{digital twins}: live software copies of physical devices such as a solar panel, a battery, or a heater, kept in sync for monitoring and control. Setting up such a twin is today mostly manual: an engineer hand-writes a configuration whose field names are arbitrary, whose units are implicit, and which carries no machine-readable description of how devices relate, so other systems cannot reliably interpret it. This position paper argues that the task should be split across three complementary tools, each doing what it does best: a modelling language for the system's structure (SysML), a standard energy vocabulary for its meaning (SAREF4ENER), and a digital-twin platform for the running representation (Eclipse Ditto, via the W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description). We give conceptual rules (not yet an executable generator) for deriving a Ditto configuration from a SysML model annotated with that vocabulary, illustrate them on a greenhouse energy-community node modelled in Modelio, and argue that the split improves traceability and configuration consistency, and establishes the semantic hooks needed for interoperability, over hand-written alternatives. We scope the contribution to design-time configuration and discuss its limits.

</details>


### [9] [An Empirical Analysis of CodeQL False Positives and Query Refinements for Java Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04535)
*Amirali Sajadi,Saikat Dutta,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究CodeQL Java安全分析中的重复性误报模式，并通过查询级精化与代理编码工具降低误报及人工分诊成本。人工构建的五类误报模式覆盖主要误报来源；精化可移除大部分已审查误报，并在跨项目适配中显示出一定效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST能够在部署前发现安全问题，但大量误报会带来显著的人工审查负担。研究旨在识别CodeQL误报中可重复、可解释的模式，评估查询级精化的降误报能力，并解决精化规则难以泛化到新项目的问题。

Method: 在110个项目的167个CVE实例上运行CodeQL Java安全查询套件，重点分析误报率最高的10个查询。研究人员人工审查500条抽样误报路径和位置，构建源代码级误报分类法；随后依据分类结果实现查询级CodeQL精化，并在完整数据集上评估其效果。进一步以误报模式模板为指导，测试两种代理编码工具将精化规则适配到新项目的能力，并比较有无模板指导时的任务成功率和查询编译通过率。

Result: 误报分为五类：遗漏路径约束或清洗（36.6%）、良性执行上下文（29.4%）、信任边界建模缺失（27.6%）、并发建模不精确（5%）以及汇建模不精确（1.4%）。查询精化移除了81.8%的已审查误报；在完整选定查询数据集中，移除15.8%的报告路径和位置，同时保留8个真实阳性中的7个。使用误报模式模板时，两种代理工具的任务成功率分别为56%和62%，查询编译通过率均超过90%；无模板指导时，成功率均为28%，编译通过率降至30%至36%。

Conclusion: CodeQL中的许多误报源于可重复的源代码和分析建模模式，可通过查询级精化有效减少，但精化规则通常依赖项目特定上下文。将模式抽象为模板并交由代理编码工具适配新项目，能够提升任务成功率和编译可靠性。研究支持一种以误报精化为核心的SAST工作流，以减少重复人工分诊。

Abstract: Static application security testing (SAST) tools help developers find vulnerabilities before deployment, but false positives create substantial triage effort. We study whether CodeQL false positives in Java security analysis form recurring, explainable patterns that can be reduced by refining the analysis. We run CodeQL's Java security query suite on 167 CVE instances from 110 projects, focusing on the ten queries with the highest false positive rates. We manually review 500 sampled false positive paths and locations and construct a source-level taxonomy. The five categories are Missed Path Constraint or Sanitization (36.6%), Benign Execution Context (29.4%), Missing Trust Boundary Modeling (27.6%), Imprecise Concurrency Modeling (5%), and Imprecise Sink Modeling (1.4%).
  Guided by these findings, we implement CodeQL refinements that detect and filter recurring false positive patterns at the query level. The refinements remove 81.8% of reviewed false positives. Across the full selected-query dataset, they remove 15.8% of reported paths and locations while retaining 7 of 8 true positives. This shows that many false positives can be reduced in the analysis, although fixed refinements often depend on project-specific context. To address this generalization gap, we evaluate whether agentic coding tools can adapt refinement patterns to new projects. Given our patterns as templates, the two tools succeed on 56% and 62% of tasks, with query compile-pass rates above 90%. Without this guidance, both succeed on only 28%, while compile rates fall to 30-36%. These results support a refinement-oriented SAST workflow in which recurring false positives are modeled in CodeQL queries and automatically adapted to different project contexts, reducing repeated triage.

</details>


### [10] [A Mixed-Method Empirical Study of LLM Assistance in Software Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04214)
*Pamali D. Weerasinghe,Roshan N. Rajapakse,Isuru Dharmadasa,Chamath Keppitiyagama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文采用混合方法研究LLM辅助软件工程对不同年级本科生的影响，发现其效果取决于任务类型、学习者经验与验证实践，而非单纯取决于代码生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将LLM辅助开发的效果笼统化，较少区分任务类型、开发者资历以及验证要求。本文旨在比较一年级与四年级学生在不同软件工程任务中的LLM使用、信任校准、工作流变化与表现差异。

Method: 研究分为两阶段：第一阶段对157名学生开展问卷调查，考察LLM接触、依赖程度与信任校准；第二阶段对20名学生进行任务型准实验，比较AI辅助与非AI条件下的实现、约束驱动的算法选择和架构推理任务。研究结合任务表现、屏幕录制行为轨迹及定性编码进行分析。

Result: 问卷显示LLM已被广泛采用，学生投入了较多验证 effort，不同年级对LLM处理约束密集型任务的能力认知存在差异。准实验表明，AI辅助显著改变了工作流，常见模式包括优先询问AI、复制并迁移生成内容以及借助AI调试；非AI条件下则更多依赖文档、既有模板和反复试错。

Conclusion: LLM辅助软件工程的收益具有明显任务依赖性，并受到学习者经验和验证实践的共同调节。有效使用LLM不能仅关注生成速度，还应重视任务约束、架构推理能力以及对生成结果的系统验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their effects are often discussed without distinguishing between task types, developer seniority, and verification demands. This paper presents a mixed-method empirical study of LLM-assisted software engineering with first-year and fourth-year undergraduates. Phase 1 is a preliminary survey (n=157) that characterizes LLM exposure, reliance, and trust calibration among the two groups. Phase 2 is a task-based quasi-experiment with a purposive sample from both cohorts (n=20). Here, we compare AI-assisted and non-AI conditions on a structured set of software engineering tasks spanning implementation, constraint-driven algorithm selection, and architectural reasoning. We then analyze performance outcomes alongside behavioral traces captured via screen recording and a qualitative coding process. Survey results indicate widespread LLM adoption and substantial verification effort, alongside cohort differences in perceived LLM capability for constraint-heavy scenarios. The quasi-experiment further shows that AI assistance changes workflow structure. For example, participants frequently adopt AI-first task entry, copy-transfer integration, and AI-mediated debugging, whereas non-AI workflows rely more on documentation, prior templates, and iterative trial-error refinement. Overall, our findings suggest that the benefits of LLM assistance are task-dependent and mediated by expertise and verification practices, rather than by generation speed alone.

</details>


### [11] [A systematic literature review on logging smell detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04215)
*Nora Madi,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统性文献综述梳理了21项日志异味自动检测研究，指出该领域在异味定义、检测范围、数据集与评估方法上缺乏统一标准，导致不同研究难以横向比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志对软件监控、行为理解和故障排查至关重要，但不恰当的日志实践会形成日志异味，降低日志可读性、诊断价值或运行可靠性。现有自动检测研究分散，亟需系统总结其检测对象、技术路线、数据资源及验证方式。

Method: 作者采用系统性文献综述方法，筛选并分析了21篇聚焦日志异味检测的研究。具体包括：界定日志及日志异味相关术语；将研究涉及的异味类型映射至既有分类体系；归纳各研究采用的自动检测技术；比较所用数据集、项目来源和评估指标；分析不同研究的覆盖范围与实验验证策略。

Result: 研究发现，日志异味检测尚未形成一致的研究范式。不同工作针对的异味种类和数量存在明显差异，对同类异味的处理方式也不统一。检测技术、数据来源和评估流程较为分散，尤其缺少公共基准数据集和标准化评价协议，因此难以公平、可靠地比较不同方法的性能与适用性。

Conclusion: 日志异味自动检测仍处于需要规范化和体系化发展的阶段。未来应建立统一的术语与异味分类标准，构建具有代表性、可复用的公开数据集和基准任务，并采用一致的评价指标与实验协议。论文还指出，可进一步开发实用检测工具，并探索利用大语言模型增强异味识别、上下文理解和修复建议能力。

Abstract: Context:Logging is an important part of software development that helps developers monitor systems, understand behavior, and fix problems. But when logging is done poorly, it can introduce logging smells, which are defects that reduce the usefulness of logs or even make them problematic. Objective:This study looks at how logging smells are currently detected. The goal is to better understand the existing research on automatic detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation methods. Method:We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) of 21 studies focused on detecting logging smells. In this review, we define key logging-related terms, identify and map the types of smells to an existing taxonomy, and examine the detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation strategies used across the studies. Results:We found that the research is still scattered and inconsistent. For example, there is no common benchmark or standardized approach for evaluating results, making it difficult to compare studies. In addition, we observe inconsistencies in the way log smells are addressed, as studies differ in the types and number of smells they target. Conclusion:There is still room for improvement in how logging smells are studied and detected. We point out several challenges and suggest future directions, such as developing better tools, using large language models (LLMs), and building more standardized datasets for evaluation.

</details>


### [12] [Engineering as Code: Bringing Software Engineering Methodology to Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04216)
*Song Difei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出“工程即代码”（Engineering as Code, EaC）范式，主张工程设计智能化的主要瓶颈在于缺乏可计算、可验证和可版本化的设计表示，而非大语言模型能力不足。论文设计了ADL语言与ESA静态分析框架，并在三个跨领域样例上验证了其表达能力和规则检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD/BIM系统将设计逻辑与几何模型紧密耦合，导致质量保证依赖后置审查；自动合规检查又容易受到几何误报、命名依赖和模型重建成本的影响。论文旨在建立一种机器可消费、验证、版本管理和复用的文本化工程设计基础，以支持工程领域的可靠AI应用。

Method: 提出EaC范式，将工程设计表达为文本原生的声明式语言，并引入规则引擎、版本控制和包管理作为质量门禁。具体构建ADL（Assembly Definition Language），以Part为基本单元，划分为PDL（部件定义）、PML（部件配合）和PLL（部件布局）三个正交子语言；同时提出ESA（Engineering Static Analysis），基于Family类型和Mate关系等语义类别进行设计时合规检查，减少对几何和命名的依赖。通过电信机架扩展、模块化数据中心和机械键盘三个样例，验证69条规则，并开展受控违规注入实验。

Result: 原型系统在三个跨领域样例上完成了ADL表达能力和ESA检测验证，69条规则的检查耗时低于200毫秒。受控违规注入实验报告100%的检测率和零误报。论文还完成了SD-HWE-Bench基准的设计，但摘要未提供其规模、基线模型或独立测试结果。

Conclusion: 研究支持“信息表示假设”：工程AI的关键限制可能源于缺乏适合机器处理的设计表示。ADL与ESA展示了将工程设计前移为可静态分析、可验证代码的可行性，但当前证据主要来自原型和有限样例，仍需通过更大规模基准、更多工程领域及与现有CAD/BIM和ACC方法的系统对比来验证泛化性与实际工程价值。

Abstract: Large language models have made significant progress in verifiable domains such as code generation and chip design, yet remain limited on engineering design tasks in fields like architecture, mechanical engineering, and HVAC. This paper argues the root cause is not model capability but the absence of a "Design as Code" computable foundation: design representations that can be consumed, verified, and versioned by machines. Existing CAD/BIM systems couple design logic with geometry, causing quality assurance to drift downstream; Automated Compliance Checking (ACC) suffers from geometric false positives and naming-dependent model reconstruction overhead. We propose the Engineering as Code (EaC) paradigm: expressing engineering design as text-native declarative language with automated rule engines, version control, and package management as quality gates. Core contributions: (1) the Information Representation Hypothesis, arguing the engineering AI bottleneck stems from missing computable design representations rather than model capability; (2) ADL (Assembly Definition Language) with Part as atom, organized into three orthogonal sub-languages -- PDL (Part Definition), PML (Part Mating), and PLL (Part Layout); (3) ESA (Engineering Static Analysis), shifting compliance checking from downstream review to design-time, operating on semantic categories (Family types, Mate relations) rather than geometry, eliminating false positives and naming dependencies. A prototype validates ADL expressiveness and ESA detection across three cross-domain samples (telecom rack expansion, modular datacenter, mechanical keyboard), checking 69 rules in under 200ms. A controlled violation injection experiment achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives. The SD-HWE-Bench benchmark design is complete as an empirical testbed for the hypothesis.

</details>


### [13] [A Governance Methodology Layer for AI-Assisted Software Development: Defect Taxonomy, Controlled Ablation, and Process-Over-Capability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04218)
*Sungjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究如何通过结构化治理流程提升自主编码代理的语义缺陷发现能力。结果显示，结构化审查在关键缺陷召回率和严重度判定一致性方面优于非结构化提示，但仍无法替代人工质量检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化流水线能够验证编译、类型安全和持续集成等语法属性，却难以发现设计边界、安全不变量及可维护性契约等语义问题。因此，论文旨在建立可移植、可执行且与具体代码生成工具解耦的审查治理机制。

Method: 论文首先基于五类AI代理权限与治理模块构建缺陷分类体系，并区分可由静态分析检测的缺陷与需要语义审查的缺陷。随后提出基于文件协议的运行时解耦治理门，以及将验证流程编码为版本控制、跨平台可执行工件的方法论即代码架构。实验采用受控消融设计，在5个工件和8项独立真实标签上，对比结构化审查框架与代币数量匹配的非结构化审查提示。

Result: 结构化条件的宽松标准真实标签召回率为62%，非结构化条件为50%；严格标准召回率分别为25%和0%。结构化审查的严重度评级更一致，而非结构化审查倾向于夸大严重程度。两种条件均未发现人工QA审查识别出的文档质量缺陷，表明AI结构化审查与人工流程检查具有互补性。

Conclusion: 在该小样本范围内，结果支持以下判断：影响AI辅助软件开发关键缺陷覆盖率的主要因素可能是审查流程设计，而非审查模型本身的能力。所提出的治理协议和方法论即代码为跨工具部署提供了可行框架，但结论仍需通过更大规模、多样化工件和独立模型实验验证。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents produce output that passes syntactic checks -- compilation, type safety, CI -- at high velocity. Yet syntactic correctness does not imply semantic correctness: design boundaries, security invariants, and maintainability contracts remain structurally invisible to automated pipelines. This paper makes four contributions toward closing this gap. First, we present a defect-class taxonomy grounded in five AI agent permission and governance modules, distinguishing defects structurally detectable by static analysis from those requiring semantic review. Second, we describe a runtime-decoupled governance gate -- a file-based protocol that reads generator output and emits a structured verdict without API coupling, making it portable across code-generation tools. Third, we formalize methodology-as-code: expressing a verification protocol as a version-controlled, executable, cross-platform artifact with a two-layer architecture separating portable methodology from host-specific automation. Fourth, we report a controlled ablation experiment (E-ablation, N=5 artifacts, 8-item independent ground truth) comparing harness-structured review against a token-matched unstructured review prompt on the same artifacts. The structured condition records 62% lenient ground-truth recall against 50% for the unstructured condition, with 25% strict recall against 0%, and applies more consistent severity grades where the unstructured condition inflates severity. Both conditions miss document-quality defects identified by a human QA reviewer, indicating a complementarity between structured AI review and human process inspection. Within the bounds of this sample, the results are consistent with the thesis that review-process design, rather than reviewer-model capability, is the dominant factor in critical-defect coverage for AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [14] [Large Language Models for Fuzz Testing in Microservices: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04219)
*Ying Song,Ke Ping,Yuqing Wang,Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统性文献综述，梳理了2024—2026年间20项关于大语言模型辅助微服务模糊测试的研究，归纳其应用角色、集成方式、评估实践与未解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务API具有异构性、输入组合空间大及服务状态依赖复杂等特点，传统模糊测试难以高效生成语义有效且满足调用约束的测试输入。尽管大语言模型可利用规范、上下文和运行反馈提升测试能力，现有研究的技术路线与效果缺乏系统整合，因此需要开展综述以识别共识、差距和未来方向。

Method: 研究遵循系统性文献综述流程，筛选并分析20篇主要研究，重点考察：大语言模型在模糊测试中的功能定位，例如语义输入生成；与测试框架的集成架构，例如黑盒、智能体和检索增强方案；实验对象、指标、基准及成本报告方式；以及研究中提出的现实部署挑战。

Result: 结果表明，大语言模型最常被用于黑盒模糊测试中的语义输入生成，以提高输入合法性和对API规范、状态依赖的适配能力。研究架构正逐步从直接提示生成转向智能体式工作流和检索增强生成。已有实验通常报告了有效输入比例提升，以及覆盖率和缺陷发现能力的适度改善；但不同研究采用的目标系统、指标和基准差异较大，因而横向比较困难。此外，成本、延迟与资源消耗报告不足，实验对象也多集中于单服务场景，未充分反映多服务协同环境的复杂性。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是建立大语言模型角色与集成方式的分类体系，汇总现有评估实践，并将关键挑战映射为后续研究方向。未来应优先构建标准化、可复现的微服务测试基准，纳入多服务调用链和状态场景，统一有效性、覆盖率、缺陷发现率及成本等评估指标，并系统研究智能体和检索增强方案在真实工程环境中的可靠性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Microservice systems (MSS) increasingly rely on heterogeneous APIs whose combinatorial input space and stateful dependencies challenge traditional fuzz testing. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced to enhance fuzzing with semantic reasoning over specifications, inputs, and runtime feedback. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) of LLM-assisted fuzz testing for microservices to synthesise how LLMs are applied, evaluated, and what challenges remain. Following established SLR guidelines, we analyze 20 primary studies published between 2024 and 2026. Results show LLMs are mainly used as semantic input generators in black-box fuzzing, with a growing shift towards agent-based and retrieval-augmented architectures, improving valid input generation and modestly increasing coverage and vulnerability detection. However, evaluation remains heterogeneous, with limited benchmark standardization, scarce cost reporting, and a bias toward single-service experiments, highlighting a gap with real-world multi-service systems. This review provides a taxonomy of LLM roles and integrations, a consolidated view of evaluation practices, and a mapping of open challenges to research directions, supporting the design and deployment of LLM-driven fuzzing in microservices.

</details>


### [15] [Robustness and Trade-offs for Code LLMs on Protected Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04220)
*Jin Wen,Yuejun Guo,Yujie Ma,Qiang Hu,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过执行测试系统评估了七种代码大语言模型在多种代码混淆条件下的翻译与补全能力。结果表明，直接处理混淆代码通常不劣于先反混淆再推理，且高能力模型在混淆输入上的Pass@1约达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反混淆通常被视为代码大模型下游分析前的必要预处理，但其对不同模型和代码保护方法的实际收益尚未得到系统验证。本文旨在评估反混淆是否确实能够提升代码大模型性能，并为受保护代码工作流提供依据。

Method: 研究覆盖七种代码大模型、C++、Go、Java和JavaScript四种源语言、五种混淆方法，以及普通输入、混淆输入和反混淆输入三种推理协议。通过执行结果评估代码翻译与补全性能，并比较模型能力、源语言和混淆方法的影响。

Result: 直接在混淆代码上推理通常达到或超过对恢复代码推理的效果。GPT-4.1和Qwen3-Coder-30B等高能力模型在混淆翻译输入上的Pass@1约为90%。同模型反混淆能够修复部分失败案例，但也会使许多原本成功的案例退化；低能力模型的净损失最明显。模型能力是最主要因素，源语言和混淆方法具有次要但稳定的影响。

Conclusion: 反混淆并非代码大模型流程中的普遍必要步骤，应根据模型能力和具体保护方法进行模型感知的流程设计。受保护代码任务应优先采用基于执行的指标进行评估，而不应仅依赖静态相似度。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used on software artifacts that may be intentionally obfuscated for intellectual-property protection, reverse-engineering resistance, or controlled access. In reverse engineering and security analysis, deobfuscation is commonly treated as the preprocessing step before downstream analysis or inference, yet its utility for code LLM pipelines has not been systematically validated across models and protection methods. We present an execution-based study of seven code LLMs on protected-code translation and completion, spanning source programs from C++, Go, Java, and JavaScript, five obfuscation methods, and three inference protocols: plain, obfuscated, and deobfuscated. Our results show that direct inference on obfuscated code often matches or exceeds inference on restored code. In this controlled benchmark, higher-capability models such as GPT-4.1 and Qwen3-Coder-30B retain about 90% Pass@1 on obfuscated translation inputs, indicating that explicit restoration is often unnecessary. Same-model restoration does recover some obfuscation-induced failures, but it also degrades many cases that already succeed, with lower-capability models showing the largest net losses. Across settings, model capability is the primary factor, while source language and obfuscation method have secondary but consistent effects. Overall, our findings support model-aware pipeline design and indicate that protected-code workflows should be evaluated primarily with execution-based metrics rather than static similarity alone.

</details>


### [16] [Automated Deployment of Real-Time Tasks for Phased Execution on Scratchpad-Based Multicore Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04221)
*Konstantin Dudzik,Maximilian Kirschner,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多核实时系统的模型驱动部署方法，用于自动将应用适配为分阶段执行模式，以降低共享内存争用带来的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能多核平台上的功能整合提升了并行执行需求，但多个核心竞争访问共享内存会产生不可预测的时序干扰。分阶段执行可通过互斥的内存访问阶段缓解该问题，但其建模、管理与调度复杂，阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 方法构建了集成式工具链：部署工具从生成的应用二进制文件中直接提取所需信息，自动扩展应用以插入必要的内存访问阶段；同时提供运行时环境，在目标硬件上协调各阶段的执行。该流程以模型驱动方式支持应用部署，并尽量减少对原有应用实现的修改。

Result: 作者在一个基于RISC-V多核系统的航空电子案例中验证了该方法，展示了工具链、阶段扩展机制和运行时编排的关键组成部分及运行原理。摘要未提供具体的性能指标、时延界限或与基线方案的定量对比。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是降低分阶段执行的工程使用门槛：通过二进制级信息提取、自动化阶段部署和运行时协调，将共享内存干扰控制机制纳入较完整的应用部署流程。其实际效果仍需结合完整论文中的开销、可扩展性、调度可行性及定量实验结果进一步评估。

Abstract: The increasing throughput demands in real-time systems and the consolidation of functionality on few, high-performance platforms are driving a shift towards parallel architectures. A key challenge for multicore real-time systems is the interference from contention for access to shared memory. Phased execution approaches address this challenge by restricting shared memory access to mutually exclusive memory phases, while computation is limited to memories local to each core. However, the complexity of managing and scheduling said memory phases hinders the adoption of phased execution in real-time applications. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based deployment methodology that automates the process of adapting applications to phased execution. We present an integrated tool-based deployment process that extends the application through the necessary memory phases and provides a runtime environment to orchestrate execution on the target hardware platform. Our approach requires minimal changes to the application implementation since the deployment tool directly extracts the required information from the generated application binaries. We validate our methodology using an avionics case study on a RISC-V multicore system, showcasing its key components and principles of operation.

</details>


### [17] [Physics-Direct FPGA Tooth-Contact Computation for Deterministic Gear Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04248)
*Jiacheng Miao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向齿轮啮合接触估计的“physics-direct” FPGA 加速器，将接触点定位、主曲率提取和椭圆赫兹接触解析模型直接硬件化，实现无需训练、确定性且可嵌入的实时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加载齿面接触分析（LTCA）本质上是数据相关的线性互补问题，在双精度下每个啮合相位计算耗时较长，难以满足数字孪生、硬件在环和主动振动控制的实时性与确定性要求。神经网络代理模型虽推理快速，但训练样本仍需由LTCA生成，且超出训练分布后的可靠性缺乏保证。

Method: 作者将闭式接触物理过程压缩为无分支定点 FPGA 数据通路，包括接触点定位、主曲率计算和椭圆赫兹解。系统基于完全开源的 openXC7 流程实现，不依赖厂商工具和浮点 IP，并在 XC7K480T 器件上验证。论文实现了几何、赫兹及两种融合路径共四类 SoC，通过 JTAG 回读与黄金模型进行逐位一致性校验。

Result: 硬件预览结果显示，主体接触压力相对 LCP 的误差约为 -22%，加载传递误差（LTE）峰峰值误差约为 -19%，同时保持精确的 W^(1/3) 载荷标度关系。在无需训练的情况下，其载荷外推误差为18.9%，优于受训神经网络的20.3%。计算延迟为编译期常数，抖动σ为0；受 DSP 资源限制，几何内核可扩展至约63条并行通道。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，直接硬件化解析接触物理能够在保持可解释性、确定性和零训练外推能力的同时，实现适合嵌入式齿轮接触估计的高吞吐计算。其主要局限是结果属于基于闭式赫兹近似的接触预览，精度相对完整 LCP-LTCA 仍存在约20%的偏差，且摘要未说明复杂齿面误差、摩擦、非赫兹接触或更广泛工况下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Gear digital twins, hardware-in-the-loop rigs, and active vibration control close a loop around the instantaneous tooth-contact state, demanding a solver that is real-time, deterministic, and embeddable -- properties that loaded tooth contact analysis (LTCA), a data-dependent linear complementarity problem (LCP) costing seconds per mesh phase in fp64, structurally lacks; learned surrogates infer fast but spend one full LTCA solve per training sample and offer no guarantee beyond their training envelope. We instead compress the closed-form contact physics -- contact-point localization, principal-curvature extraction, and the elliptical Hertz solution -- directly into a branch-free fixed-point FPGA datapath, termed physics-direct, realized through the fully open-source openXC7 flow (no vendor tools, no floating-point IP) and validated on retired XC7K480T silicon. Four SoCs -- geometry, Hertz, and two fused paths -- pass bit-exact JTAG readback against a golden model. The on-chip preview tracks LCP body pressure to within -22% and loaded transmission error (LTE) peak-to-peak to -19%, preserving the exact $W^{1/3}$ law; with zero training it extrapolates in load more accurately than a trained network (18.9% versus 20.3%). Latency is a compile-time constant ($σ= 0$ jitter), and the DSP-bound geometry kernel saturates the fabric near 63 lanes. Physics-direct is an interpretable, deterministic alternative to neural surrogacy for embedded tooth-contact estimation.

</details>


### [18] [Reviewer Capability Governs Rejection Targeting, Not Repair Skill: Evidence from LLM Execute-Review-Revise Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04270)
*Faizan Tanveer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，验证器的能力与其效果密切相关：跨模型的中等能力验证器将最终准确率从52%提升至64%，且未造成答案损坏；同模型自我审查虽有较高错误检测率，却因过度拒绝和低修复率未带来显著收益；能力过低的验证器则基本不起作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型系统常将执行与验证分工，以降低始终使用旗舰模型的成本。但既有研究通常将验证器能力相对固定，尚未充分考察不同能力层级验证器对最终结果、误拒绝和答案损坏的影响。

Method: 在固定的100道奥林匹克数学题上，使用同一执行器，并将验证器替换为跨能力范围、包括无法独立解题的多种模型。研究逐题记录验证器拒绝、执行器修复及最终答案变化，比较跨模型验证、同模型自我审查和低能力验证器的效果，并进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 中等能力的跨模型验证器使准确率由52%提高至64%，提升12个百分点（p=0.0005），且没有损坏答案。同模型自我审查的错误召回率最高（0.85），但拒绝次数是跨模型验证器的2.1倍，修复率仅15%而非43%（p=0.0074），并错误拒绝了自身35%的正确答案，相比跨模型验证器的2%（配对p=0.000015）。自我审查的低损坏率主要源于执行器的修订惰性：18个被错误拒绝的正确答案中，执行器采纳意见的3个全部变错，忽略意见的15个保持正确。最弱验证器使100个答案均未发生变化，却使令牌成本翻倍。

Conclusion: 验证阶段并非天然有效，其收益取决于验证器的能力层级及其与执行器的关系。中等能力、跨模型的验证器在该设置下最有效；同模型自我审查容易产生过度拒绝和低效修复，过弱验证器则仅增加成本。由于实验仅覆盖单一执行器—验证器配置和100道题目，结论应视为受控试点结果，不能直接推广至所有验证流程。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines increasingly assign roles, including execution and verification, to models of different capability tiers. This is done because running a flagship model at every stage is expensive. Previous literature has established that verification stages are not always beneficial, but holds reviewer capability roughly fixed relative to the executor. We vary it. We replace the reviewer with models spanning a capability range down to one that cannot solve the problems at all, and measure the outcome of every individual rejection. This is done across a constant set of 100 olympiad mathematics problems.
  A cross-family mid-tier reviewer improves final accuracy by 12 percentage points, from 52 to 64 percent (p = 0.0005), with zero damaged answers. Same-model self-review attains the highest error-detection rate of any condition (0.85 recall) yet yields no significant gain: it rejects 2.1 times as often for a third the repair rate (15 against 43 percent, p = 0.0074) and falsely rejects 35 percent of its own correct answers against 2 percent for the cross-family reviewer (paired p = 0.000015). The low damage rate of self-review proves to be an artifact of revision inertia rather than reviewer quality: of 18 falsely rejected correct answers, the three where the executor complied all became wrong, while the fifteen it ignored survived unchanged. Below a capability floor the role becomes inert: our weakest reviewer changed zero of 100 final answers while doubling token cost. These findings describe a single executor-reviewer configuration on 100 problems and should be read as a controlled pilot rather than a general claim about verification stages.

</details>


### [19] [Integrating Crash Report Mining and LLMs for Bug Localization and Repair: An Industrial Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04483)
*Marcos Medeiros,Uirá Kulesza,Christoph Treude,Daniel Lucena,Rafael Gomes,Roberta Coelho,Eiji Adachi,Rodrigo Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了将崩溃报告挖掘技术与大语言模型结合，用于大型工业软件中崩溃缺陷定位与修复的效果。最佳配置在完整数据集上可定位71%的崩溃缺陷，并正确修复52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业软件的崩溃报告数量大、关联源码复杂，开发者需要投入大量维护工作以定位根因并修复缺陷。现有研究虽表明大语言模型可辅助软件维护，但其对基于成组崩溃报告进行定位和修复的实际有效性尚不明确。

Method: 研究将栈追踪聚类、可疑文件排序和可疑方法排序等崩溃报告挖掘技术与大语言模型结合。作者先在四种提示配置下回顾性评估五个模型，再选取最佳模型，对来自两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃缺陷进行分析；同时通过人工验证评估定位与修复结果，并分析模型回答的结构特征和解释模式。

Result: 最佳模型与提示配置能够在完整数据集上定位最多71%的崩溃缺陷，并对52%的缺陷给出正确修复。结果显示，崩溃报告挖掘所提供的聚类和可疑代码排序信息能够增强大语言模型对故障上下文的理解，并提高其生成定位和修复建议的可用性。

Conclusion: 将崩溃报告挖掘与大语言模型结合，是支持工业维护流程中崩溃缺陷调试的有效方法。该方案在缺陷定位方面表现较强，但修复正确率仍有提升空间，适合作为开发者的辅助工具而非完全自动化替代方案。

Abstract: Analyzing crash-report bugs in large-scale industrial software systems requires substantial maintenance effort, particularly in production environments where developers must handle large volumes of crash reports and source code artifacts to localize and fix their root causes. While recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) can assist with maintenance tasks, little is known about their effectiveness in supporting developers in analyzing crash-report bugs and repairing bugs associated with groups of crash reports in industrial settings. To address this gap, we investigate whether integrating crash report mining techniques---specifically stack trace clustering and suspicious file and method ranking---with LLMs can support crash localization and repair in production environments. We conduct a retrospective evaluation of five LLMs under four prompt configurations. After that, we chose the best model to run on 38 crash bugs collected from two large Java enterprise systems. We further analyze the structural characteristics and explanatory patterns of LLM-generated responses and assess localization and repair effectiveness through manual validation. Our results show that the best-performing configuration localizes up to 71% and correctly repairs 52% of crash bugs on the full dataset. These findings provide empirical evidence that combining crash report mining with LLM-based repair can effectively support debugging activities in industrial maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [20] [Dynamic Adaptation of the LLM Context for Generating Routines with Coupled Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04570)
*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出动态上下文适应（Dynamic Context Adaptation, DCA），用于解决代码生成中依赖多组件联合运行行为的“静态绑定”问题。该方法通过执行轨迹驱动的结构化诊断反馈、知识图谱语义约束、多候选生成与模拟退火选择，在有限评估预算下提升程序搜索和优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的代码生成主要依赖文本规格与静态推理；当一个例程的语义或正确性取决于另一例程在运行时的实际行为时，文本信息不足以确定正确实现。此类依赖不仅存在于显式耦合优化，也广泛存在于装箱、路径规划和符号搜索等具有联合约束的问题中。现有搜索方法在300至600次评估的低预算阶段通常难以积累足够的候选多样性，因此需要更高样本效率的执行反馈机制。

Method: DCA采用验证—生成闭环：验证代理解析程序执行轨迹，提取结构化诊断信息，并将其作为近似梯度式的反馈提供给生成代理；生成代理结合由题目描述构建的知识图谱，遵守语义约束并在每轮产生多个候选解；随后使用模拟退火在候选间进行选择，以降低贪婪选择导致搜索早熟收敛的风险。论文还通过消融实验分离结构化执行反馈、知识图谱和候选选择机制的贡献。

Result: 在8个任务中，DCA在300次和600次评估预算下均有7个任务优于zero-shot、Reflexion和OpenEvolve，统计显著性为p<0.01。在主要的交叉耦合优化任务上，DCA在1000次评估时仍取得最佳分数。消融结果表明，基于执行轨迹的结构化反馈是性能提升的主要来源；这支持了其在运行时耦合决定正确性的任务上尤其有效的判断。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将代码生成从仅依赖静态文本提示，扩展为利用实际执行行为进行迭代适应。结果显示，结构化运行时诊断能够在低评估预算下显著提高搜索效率，尤其适合存在跨组件动态耦合与联合约束的问题。摘要中的局限是未说明各任务的具体规模、执行成本、知识图谱构建细节及高预算下对所有基线的稳定优势，因而方法的通用性和成本效益仍需结合全文实验进一步评估。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation fails when correctness depends on execution-dependent coupling: the meaning of one routine is defined by the runtime behavior of another, a relationship that cannot be resolved from textual descriptions alone. This limitation, which we call static binding, is not confined to explicitly coupled problems; it appears to varying degrees whenever correctness depends on joint execution behavior across components, from explicit cross-coupled optimizers to subtler joint constraints in packing, routing, and symbolic search. This paper proposes dynamic context adaptation, a sample-efficient validation-generation loop designed for this setting. A validation agent extracts structured diagnostic information from execution traces, providing gradient-like guidance to a generation agent that proposes multiple candidates per iteration. A knowledge graph derived from the problem description supplies semantic constraints to the generation agent. Simulated annealing selects among candidates to avoid greedy collapse. Our method outperforms zero-shot, Reflexion, and OpenEvolve on seven of eight problems at both 300 and 600 evaluations (p < 0.01), a regime where population-based search has not yet accumulated sufficient diversity to compete. Notably, on the primary motivating problem (cross-coupled optimization), our method also achieves the best score at 1000 evaluations, consistent with the hypothesis that structured execution feedback is most beneficial when correctness depends on runtime coupling. Ablation results confirm that structured execution feedback is the primary driver.

</details>


### [21] [Software Engineering in the Agent Era From Trustworthy Change to Human Agent Software Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04630)
*Zhongjie Wang,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关于智能体规模化软件执行如何被治理的理论框架：将可信变更（Trustworthy Change, TC）作为核心工程对象，以责任拓扑刻画最终残余风险接受权的组织分布，并以人机智能体单元（HAC）描述执行主体。核心判断是，执行能力可以快速扩展，但语义承诺、验证、整合和风险接受仍受人类认知与组织权责约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件智能体能够并行扩展代码分析、生成、测试、迁移、工具调用和运维，但组织的决策、验收和责任承载能力并未同比增长。因此，需要解释如何在执行规模化的同时保持变更可验证、责任可闭合并能够长期运行。

Method: 作者进行理论构建与操作化，定义两个构念和一个执行抽象：以TC描述从意图、委托执行、验证、整合、接受到运行的完整变更过程；以责任拓扑区分单中心拓扑和多锚点拓扑，前者具有单一最终责任基准，后者要求多个独立治理域共同接受残余风险；以HAC描述生成候选方案、提案和证据的执行单元，并明确其不具有验收权。基于执行能力与权责能力的非对称扩展，推导语境一致性、变更失效、联合验收、责任闭合、共享工程事实、验证和流量控制等问题。

Result: 框架推导出：分布式HAC在执行规模扩大时会面临语境不一致和既有结论失效压力；多锚点治理还会增加跨域联合验收及责任闭合要求。文章将责任分布明确为组织分类轴，并提出渐进式规范和有界容量分析等可检验假设，但未提供实证结果。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是重新组织既有的责任、问责、变更管理、规范、验证和人类监督概念，以适应智能体规模化执行场景，而非宣称建立已验证的管理定律。其结论仍需通过受控实验、纵向研究和现场研究检验；实践上，组织应区分执行权与验收权，明确残余风险接受者，并建立跨HAC的共享事实、验证和责任闭合机制。

Abstract: Software agents make digital execution elastic: repository analysis, code generation, testing, migration, tool use, and operations can be replicated and parallelized without proportional human headcount. Problem framing, semantic commitment, verification, integration, attention, and residual-risk acceptance remain bounded by human cognition, organizational authority, and economic capacity. How should scalable execution be governed so organizations can accept and sustain its changes?
  Our testable framework has two constructs and one execution abstraction. Trustworthy Change (TC) is the engineering object moving from intent through delegated execution, verification, integration, acceptance, and operation. Responsibility Topology classifies organizations by the distribution of independent residual-risk acceptance authority. A single-center topology has one final baseline responsibility anchor; a multi-anchor topology requires joint acceptance across independently governed domains. The Human-Agent Cell (HAC) produces candidates, proposals, and evidence; execution grants no acceptance authority. As execution and authority scale differently, distributed HACs create context-coherence and invalidation pressures, while multi-anchor governance adds joint acceptance and explicit responsibility closure.
  Responsibility, accountability, change management, specification, verification, and human oversight predate this work; our claim is only that agent-scaled execution changes how they fit together. We make that authority an explicit classification axis and derive consequences for change state, shared engineering facts, verification, and flow control. Progressive Specification and bounded-capacity analysis remain hypotheses to test, not laws. We contribute theory construction and operationalization; empirical validity remains open to controlled, longitudinal, and field studies.

</details>


### [22] [How Developers Discuss Generative AI: A Longitudinal Study of the Visual Studio Code Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04680)
*Panida Rumriankit,Akito Monden,Hiroki Inayoshi,Pattara Leelaprute,Bundit Manaskasemsak,Kundjanasith Thonglek,Arnon Rungsawang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对VS Code GitHub仓库中与生成式人工智能相关的开发者讨论进行纵向分析，发现讨论重点主要集中在AI开发工具的实际运行、配置与管理问题，而非幻觉和许可等常见风险议题；讨论主题也从代码补全逐步转向对话式和智能体驱动的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI研究较多依赖问卷调查，难以反映开发者在真实软件开发流程中的具体关注点。本文旨在利用开源社区的实际讨论，刻画生成式AI在软件开发中的应用主题及其长期演化。

Method: 研究收集了2021年1月至2026年6月期间VS Code GitHub仓库中的43,806个候选问题，结合关键词检索与语义相关性过滤，得到25,227个AI相关问题。研究采用BERTopic进行主题建模，并通过过滤语料进行主题验证和稳健性重新聚类；随后使用月度主题占比与Mann-Kendall趋势检验分析主题随时间的变化。

Result: 讨论主要聚焦智能体管理、配置、可靠性、身份认证和计费等操作性问题。问卷研究中常见的幻觉与软件许可风险在该问题追踪场景中较少出现。随着时间推移，讨论重点由AI辅助代码补全转向对话式开发和基于智能体的开发，显示生成式AI正逐步深入软件开发工作流。

Conclusion: GitHub Issues呈现出一种以实际工作流和工具运维为中心的生成式AI应用视角，可补充问卷研究对开发者态度和感知的分析。研究表明，真实社区中的主要关注点往往是工具可用性与集成管理，而不一定是抽象或预设的AI风险。

Abstract: Generative AI tools such as GitHub Copilot, ChatGPT, and coding agents have rapidly become part of everyday software development, yet little is known about how mainstream open source communities discuss them in practice. This paper presents a longitudinal analysis of generative-AI-related discussions in the Visual Studio Code (VS Code) GitHub repository, using 43,806 candidate issues created between January 2021 and June 2026. To improve corpus quality, we combined keyword retrieval with semantic relevance filtering, yielding a filtered corpus of 25,227 AI-related issues. We applied BERTopic to the retrieved corpus to identify discussion topics, using the filtered corpus for theme validation and a robustness re-clustering, and analyzed their evolution over time using monthly prevalence and Mann-Kendall trend tests. The results show that developer discussions are dominated by practical concerns regarding the operation of AI-assisted development environments, including agent management, configuration, reliability, authentication, and billing, whereas risks frequently emphasized in survey-based studies, such as hallucination and licensing, rarely surface in this venue. This suggests that discussions of generative AI in the VS Code issue tracker primarily focus on operational aspects of AI-assisted software development. Furthermore, discussions evolved from AI-assisted code completion toward conversational and agent-based development, reflecting the increasing integration of generative AI into software development workflows. These findings suggest that GitHub Issues provide a practical, workflow-oriented perspective on generative AI that complements survey-based studies of developer perceptions.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Code Generation: Reliability, Verification, and Cost Economics in the Agentic Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04681)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了软件工程智能体从代码生成工具发展为可自主执行多步骤开发任务后，对软件交付效率、质量控制和成本结构的影响。文章指出，代码产出增长并不等同于可靠软件交付，验证、集成、安全、部署和运维成为主要瓶颈，并提出以生产合格变更和控制平面为核心的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注智能体生成代码的数量或开发者活动提升，但较少衡量代码经过审查、测试、部署并稳定运行后所产生的实际价值。随着成本从固定席位费用转向令牌、工具、沙箱、持续集成和返工等可变成本，亟需建立面向端到端软件交付的效率、可靠性和经济性分析框架。

Method: 本文采用证据综合方法，汇总同行评审的软件工程研究、大学研究、基准测试审计、科技企业生产报告、开发者遥测数据及成本管理证据，主要覆盖2024年至2026年9月。文章不声称开展新的模型实验，而是保留并归因于原始研究的数值结论。在此基础上提出四个概念：智能体软件开发生命周期吞吐悖论、生产合格变更（PQC）、验证税，以及依据成本、可靠性和人力注意力预算分配自主权的智能体软件开发生命周期控制平面；同时构建从当前受监督智能体到未来受策略约束的软件工厂的演进路径。

Result: 综合证据表明，智能体能够显著提升编码活动，但从编写代码到交付可靠软件的收益会明显衰减。代码审查、集成、测试、安全、部署和生产运维仍是限制整体吞吐量的关键阶段。与此同时，智能体系统的经济模型变得更加动态，使用量、工具调用、执行环境、CI资源和返工都会形成额外成本。因此，软件工程绩效应从代码生成量转向每美元、每位审查者小时以及每单位运营风险所交付的生产合格价值。

Conclusion: 智能体的核心价值不应仅以生成代码的速度衡量，而应以端到端交付可靠软件的能力衡量。未来的重点是构建能够控制自主程度、系统化承担验证成本、协调人机审查并管理运营风险的工程控制平面。文章为评估智能体软件工厂提供了概念框架，但其结论主要依赖异质的既有证据，仍需进一步开展统一指标、可重复实验和长期生产验证。

Abstract: AI coding systems are moving from autocomplete and chat toward agents that can inspect repositories, edit multiple files, run tools, write tests, open pull requests, and work for long periods with limited supervision. This capability changes the bottleneck in software delivery. Recent field studies show meaningful gains in coding activity, but newer evidence also shows that those gains attenuate sharply between writing code and shipping reliable software. Review, integration, testing, security, deployment, and production operations remain constraining stages, while the economics are shifting from predictable per-seat licensing toward variable token, tool, sandbox, CI, and rework costs.
  This paper synthesizes peer-reviewed software-engineering research, university studies, benchmark audits, production reports from major technology companies, developer telemetry, and cost-management evidence released primarily from 2024 through September 2026. No new model experiment is claimed; numerical findings remain attributed to their original studies. The synthesis proposes four engineering concepts: the Agentic SDLC Throughput Paradox, Production-Qualified Change (PQC), the Verification Tax, and an Agentic SDLC Control Plane that allocates autonomy subject to cost, reliability, and human-attention budgets. An evidence-based horizon then maps today's supervised agents to future policy-bounded software factories. The central research question shifts from how much code an agent can generate to how much production-qualified value an engineering system can deliver per dollar, per reviewer-hour, and per unit of operational risk.

</details>


### [24] [Building a research-software catalog with a coding agent: from hackathon prototype to public deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04711)
*Kazuyoshi Yoshimi,Satoshi Terasaki,Gotai Yamada*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，生成式人工智能和编码代理能够快速构建科研软件目录及检索代理，但要实现可靠的公开部署，必须补充大量数据验证、浏览器测试、对抗性审查、监控和发布防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能加速了科研软件开发，也增加了软件发现、维护和质量保障的需求。研究旨在评估人工智能辅助构建软件门户的工程成本，并探索原型系统经验能否迁移至规模更大的人工维护门户。

Method: 研究团队在为期三天的黑客松中开发软件仓库目录，随后针对公开部署开展对抗性审查、数据质量检查、浏览器级验证和发布 safeguards。进一步在MateriApps门户中探索检索代理，将人工整理的门户元数据、外部文档、向量搜索和本地语言模型生成相结合，并分析编码代理实施过程中的工程问题。

Result: 编码代理显著提高了初始实现速度，但可靠运行需要大量额外工程。最严重的问题并非系统崩溃，而是由数据采集不完整、评估误导以及检索或预处理失败造成的静默错误；这些错误往往生成看似合理但不完整或不正确的结果。MateriApps相关结果仍处于探索阶段，结论具有初步性。

Conclusion: 人工智能辅助的软件门户必须配备明确的数据与输出验证、持续监控和重复审查机制。高质量的人工整理元数据和持续维护的文档仍不可替代。将人工策展数据、自动采集文档与基于检索的智能辅助结合，可能有助于扩展其他科研软件门户，但仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative AI and coding agents can accelerate research software development, but they also increase the need for efficient software discovery and maintenance. We developed a repository catalog during a three-day hackathon and subsequently examined the engineering required to make it suitable for public deployment, including adversarial review, data-quality checks, browser-level validation, and publication safeguards. We then explored whether the lessons learned from this prototype could be transferred to a much larger, human-curated portal, through a retrieval agent under development for MateriApps that combines curated portal metadata, external documentation, vector search, and local language-model generation. Implementation with coding agents was rapid, but achieving reliable operation required substantial additional engineering: the most consequential problems were not crashes but silent failures that produced plausible yet incomplete or incorrect outputs, arising from incomplete data acquisition, misleading assessments, and retrieval or preprocessing failures. These observations suggest that AI-assisted software portals require explicit validation, monitoring, and repeated review, and that curated metadata and maintained documentation remain essential. The MateriApps work is exploratory and remains under active development, so the observations reported for it are preliminary; a comparable combination of curated metadata, automatically collected documentation, and retrieval-based assistance may nevertheless be useful for extending other research-software portals.

</details>


### [25] [Same Request, Different Answer: Quantization Amplifies Cache-Induced Divergence in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04748)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究了前缀缓存对大模型推理可复现性的影响，发现其在权重量化后会显著放大轨迹差异：缓存启用时，16位模型有36.2%的多轮任务轨迹发生变化，4位模型升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存通常被主流推理框架默认启用，并被视为透明优化，但缓存状态未纳入请求或实验控制，可能破坏严格的可复现性。论文旨在量化这一影响并定位其根因。

Method: 在两个推理引擎和四种权重格式上，固定模型、解码参数、随机种子与请求顺序，以批大小1串行执行80轮多轮智能体工具调用任务；分别比较启用和禁用缓存的结果，并通过重复运行、改变服务器级缓存配置、调整执行顺序及恢复缓存状态进行因果定位。另使用单轮桥接实验检验轨迹差异是否影响任务结果。

Result: 禁用缓存时，800次重复执行均位级一致，表明其他非确定性来源低于0.5%。启用缓存后，重复运行会产生差异；单个服务器级提示缓存设置可使运行间差异变化37.5个百分点，执行顺序仅在该设置生效时产生影响。恢复缓存状态后，缓存路径与重新计算路径分别在40/40个样本上保持可复现，但两条路径之间仍有14个样本不同。缓存引起的轨迹变化可传递至任务结果，但未改变总体准确率。

Conclusion: 缓存推理在缓存状态固定时是确定的，但在实际使用中由于缓存状态未随请求记录且默认不重置，因而不可复现；该问题在低比特量化模型上尤为严重。严谨评测应记录、控制或禁用缓存状态，并将缓存配置纳入实验环境与复现实验协议。

Abstract: Prefix caching, in which a serving engine reuses the key and value tensors of a shared prompt prefix across requests, is enabled by default in the major open-source stacks and treated as a transparent optimization. We measure what it costs in reproducibility, and find that the cost rises sharply with weight quantization. Holding the model, decoding parameters, seed, and request order fixed, and issuing every request serially at batch size one, we ran an eighty-episode multi-turn agentic tool-use workload with caching enabled and disabled across two engines and four weight formats. Enabling the cache changed the agent's trajectory on 36.2 percent of episodes at 16-bit precision and on 75.0 percent at four-bit, a gradient that survives re-measurement under a controlled cache configuration. With caching disabled, repeated execution was bit-identical in every configuration, 0 of 800 episodes, which bounds other sources of nondeterminism at 0.5 percent. Repeated cache-enabled runs did diverge, and three experiments locate the cause: a single server-level prompt-cache setting moves run-to-run divergence by 37.5 percentage points, execution order acts only while that setting is active, and restoring cache state makes the cached and recompute paths each reproduce on 40 of 40 items while still differing from each other on 14. Cached serving is deterministic given cache state, and irreproducible in practice because that state is absent from the request and never reset by default. A single-turn bridge shows the divergence reaching task outcomes without shifting aggregate accuracy. We release the harness, logs, and analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [26] [Better Understanding, Better Fixes? A Study of Hallucination in LLM-based Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04909)
*Xuemeng Cai,Jiakun Liu,Linhan Yang,Wei Ma,Lingxiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统研究大语言模型在自动程序修复（APR）中的“幻觉”问题，指出幻觉不仅存在于最终补丁，也存在于引导补丁生成的中间理解产物。基于832个Defects4J缺陷和三类代表性模型的实验表明，修复幻觉与理解幻觉均较普遍，且测试通过并不必然意味着补丁具有真实的缺陷修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APR评估通常以补丁是否通过测试为核心，难以揭示模型在修复过程中的证据失真、错误理解和偶然测试通过等问题。论文旨在将评估视角从结果导向扩展到过程导向，分析模型是否忠实依据已有修复证据生成中间推理结果和最终补丁。

Method: 作者将幻觉定义为生成未被可用修复证据可靠支撑的补丁或中间产物，并区分为最终补丁中的修复幻觉与中间产物中的理解幻觉。理解幻觉通过三个任务评估：触发测试用例识别、代码行覆盖预测和额外测试用例生成。研究对三种代表性LLM在832个Defects4J缺陷上的表现开展自动评估与人工分析，并对812个采样修复结果进行人工归因，识别错误因果定位、错误修复策略等幻觉类型。

Result: 不同模型和设置下，仅21.0%至55.9%的生成补丁能够通过开发者编写的测试套件。虽然更准确的中间产物通常与成功修复相关，但二者并非稳定的充分关系。人工分析发现，812个采样修复中有72.7%存在修复幻觉，其中包括部分通过全部已有测试的补丁；错误因果定位和错误修复策略分别占修复幻觉的45.9%和18.5%。在理解层面，模型经常错误识别触发测试用例，在涉及分支控制流时错误预测行覆盖，并生成缺失缺陷触发条件或具有错误预期行为的额外测试用例。

Conclusion: 论文表明，测试套件通过率不足以充分衡量LLM驱动APR的可靠性。未来评估应同时验证补丁的语义正确性、因果依据和中间理解产物的真实性，并加强对测试充分性、控制流理解、缺陷触发条件及修复策略合理性的检查。该工作为构建过程可解释、证据可追溯且更可靠的LLM程序修复系统提供了评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly advanced automated program repair (APR), yet existing evaluations remain largely result-centric and provide limited insight into hallucination during repair. In APR, hallucination may arise not only in final patches but also in the intermediate artifacts that guide patch generation. To address this gap, we perform a multi-layered analysis of hallucination throughout the APR process. Specifically, we characterize hallucination as the production of patches or intermediate artifacts that are not faithfully grounded in the available repair evidence. We examine repair hallucination in final patches and understanding hallucination in intermediate artifacts through three tasks, namely triggering testcase identification, line coverage prediction, and additional testcase generation.We then evaluate three representative LLMs on 832 Defects4J bugs through automatic evaluation and manual analysis. Our results show that both repair and understanding hallucinations remain prevalent. Across models and settings, only 21.0%-55.9% of generated patches pass the developer-written test suite. Moreover, although more accurate intermediate artifacts are generally associated with successful repairs, this relationship does not always hold. Manual analysis of 812 sampled repairs identifies repair hallucinations in 72.7% of cases, including patches that pass all available tests; incorrect causal localization and incorrect repair strategies account for 45.9% and 18.5% of these hallucinations, respectively. Meanwhile, models frequently misidentify triggering testcases, mispredict line coverage involving branching control flow, and generate additional testcases with missing bug-triggering conditions or incorrect expected behavior.

</details>


### [27] [ARIA - An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Testing of Infotainment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04913)
*António Azevedo,Bruno Lima,João Pascoal Faria*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARIA是一种面向Android车载信息娱乐系统的多智能体大语言模型测试框架，通过视觉交互自主执行端到端测试，并生成判定结果、可复现脚本和逐步视觉证据。在30个场景中完成28个，识别全部5个已知缺陷，但存在较高误报，表明其具备工业应用潜力，同时仍需提升导航、图像理解和手势支持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载信息娱乐系统验证仍高度依赖耗时、昂贵且难以适应敏捷开发与OTA更新的人工测试。传统脚本自动化将测试逻辑与具体实现耦合，维护成本高；现有大语言模型测试框架多面向网页或移动应用，并由单个或少量智能体同时承担感知、规划、操作和验证任务，容易产生幻觉和无效探索。

Method: ARIA采用多智能体、闭环测试流程，为每个测试步骤分配四类专用智能体，分别支持视觉感知、规划、动作执行和结果验证，并设置独立报告阶段。系统从包含路径、动作和预期结果的单句场景描述出发，在实体Android车载系统上通过视觉交互执行测试，同时输出测试报告、可复现脚本及逐步视觉证据。研究还与单智能体基线进行比较，并分析模型复访、重复运行、调用次数、Token消耗和成本。

Result: 在制造商实体车载系统的30个测试场景中，ARIA完成28个并给出判定，完成率为93.3%；其中20个结果与真实标签一致，占全部场景的71.4%。系统检出全部5个已知缺陷，未将故障误判为正常，但产生8个误报，主要源于导航和图像理解限制以及不支持的手势。单智能体基线首轮误报率为72.0%，ARIA为52.6%；经更强模型复访后差距缩小。重复实验显示稳定性随场景复杂度下降，但缺陷检测保持完全一致。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构能够有效分解车载信息娱乐测试中的感知、规划、执行和验证任务，支持较高程度的自主化和可审计的视觉测试流程，并具备接入持续集成环境的潜力。当前主要瓶颈是误报率偏高，尤其受复杂导航、视觉理解和手势覆盖不足影响；后续应增强交互能力、降低复访与模型调用成本，并改进复杂场景下的判定可靠性。

Abstract: Automotive infotainment validation still relies on manual testing, slow, costly, and incompatible with agile releases and OTA updates. Scripted automation only partly helps: it couples test logic to implementation, yielding brittle, high-maintenance suites. Existing LLM-driven frameworks mostly target web/mobile apps, using single- or dual-agent setups that overload one or two models with perception, planning, action selection, and validation at once, prone to hallucinations and unproductive exploration loops given infotainment complexity. We present ARIA (Autonomous Real-time Infotainment Assessment), a multi-agent LLM framework that autonomously runs end-to-end tests on Android infotainment systems via visual interaction, using a closed-loop pipeline of four specialized agents per step plus a report stage. From single-sentence scenarios (path, action, expected outcome), ARIA runs the interactions and produces reports, reproducible scripts, and visual evidence per step. Evaluated on a manufacturer's physical Android infotainment system across 30 scenarios, ARIA completed 28 (93.3%) with a verdict (2 errored), 20 of which (71.4%) matched ground truth. It caught all 5 known defects, no fault passed as working; its 8 false positives stem from navigation/image limits and unsupported gestures, showing multi-agent LLMs can run infotainment tests industrially while exposing the cost of a low false-positive tolerance. A single-agent baseline confirms the multi-agent design's value: on the first pass, before stronger-model revisitation narrows the gap, it shows a far higher false-positive rate (72.0% vs. 52.6%), conflating navigational difficulty with system failure. We report first-pass/post-revisitation results, token/call/cost per scenario, and show via repeated runs that stability tracks complexity, with fault detection perfectly consistent, pointing to CI integration of visual testing.

</details>


### [28] [An Empirical Study on Learning Paths and Gender Dynamics in Scrum Master Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05186)
*Manuela Petrescu,Paul Razvan Petrescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多元文化软件公司中Scrum Master的职业学习路径、团队规模适配性及软技能发展评估方式。研究发现，团队规模约达到六人时通常需要Scrum Master，但行业尚未形成统一的软技能学习流程或度量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷开发在业界广泛应用，Scrum Master等相关岗位对有经验人才的需求持续较高。然而，Scrum Master的成长路径、岗位与团队规模的关系，以及软技能如何培养和衡量，仍缺乏系统性认识。

Method: 研究分两个阶段开展，采用两项定性调查和访谈研究，并对数据进行定性与定量分析，考察Scrum Master的学习过程、职业发展路径、团队规模需求及企业对软技能发展的监测方式。

Result: Scrum Master的需求与团队规模相关，研究结果表明六人可能是是否需要专职Scrum Master的重要界限。企业普遍没有标准化的软技能学习流程或统一的进展度量指标。部分企业依据客户反馈或团队反馈评估软技能，另一些企业综合两类反馈。许多学习活动，尤其是面向Scrum Master软技能的培养，主要由员工主动发起和推动。

Conclusion: Scrum Master岗位需求具有团队规模依赖性，但六人界限仍需通过更大样本和不同组织情境进一步验证。当前企业在软技能培养与评估方面缺乏统一机制，未来可建立结合客户反馈、团队反馈和员工自我发展的结构化学习路径及可操作度量体系。

Abstract: Context: Agile development methodology has been widely adopted by industry and the demand for experienced professionals in Agile-related roles is persistently high. Objectives: We focus on the learning path for a Scrum Master role in multicultural software companies and investigate the role in relation to team size, together with the learning process for a career path, and how companies monitor soft skills development. Method: We conducted our study in two phases, two qualitative surveys (interview studies) and performed a qualitative and quantitative data analysis of the results. Conclusions: Our results identified that the need for a Scrum Master (SM) depends on the size of the team, with our study indicating a six-member limit. There is no overall standardized process for soft skills learning or metrics to measure progress. Some companies measure soft skills based on feedback received from the client or from the team, and other companies are taking both types of feedback into consideration. Many learning initiatives, especially on soft skills for an SM role, were based on actions of the employees.
  The version of Record of this contribution is published in Software Engineering and Advanced Applications. SEAA 2025. Lecture Notes in Computer Science, vol 16083. Springer, Cham. and available online at: check DOI.

</details>


### [29] [T(r)opical Islands: Visualizing & Understanding Socio-Technical Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05254)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Vít Rusňák,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向协作式软件开发项目的三维可视化方法，将分散在缺陷报告、功能请求和论坛帖子等社会技术工件中的主题映射为动态“植被岛屿”，以帮助理解项目讨论内容及其随时间的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件项目中的非代码工件记录了需求、问题、设计决策和社区互动，但它们分布于多个渠道、以自然语言书写且噪声较多，导致研究者和开发者难以系统追踪跨渠道主题、识别讨论热点及理解其生命周期。

Method: 作者构建了从数据挖掘到交互式渲染的完整原型管线：收集项目社会技术工件，利用机器学习对文本工件进行聚类和主题提取，再以三维岛屿隐喻进行呈现。不同主题对应不同岛屿，单个工件被表示为不同类型的树木；主题讨论增多时岛屿从海面升起，讨论减少或停止时岛屿逐渐下沉，从而编码主题热度及时间变化。

Result: 通过多个案例研究，作者展示了该方法能够揭示项目历史中开发主题的讨论轨迹、活跃程度和演化关系。包含34名参与者的用户研究进一步表明，该可视化对理解社会技术工件及其演变具有较好的适用性和可用性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将机器学习驱动的主题分析与时间感知的三维可视化结合，可有效缓解软件项目非代码信息碎片化的问题。其主要贡献是提供了一种直观探索跨渠道项目讨论及主题生命周期的工具；但摘要未说明主题模型的精度、不同项目规模下的性能，以及三维隐喻相对于二维替代方案的量化优势。

Abstract: Projects hosted on collaborative software development platforms, such as GitHub, include many non-code artifacts documenting the project's lifecycle, with its challenges, plans, design, and even community. These socio-technical artifacts include, for example, bug reports, feature requests, and forum posts, offering a useful prospect on the project's evolution. However, these artifacts are dispersed over multiple communication channels and written in natural language, making their analysis difficult, as they are fragmented and with considerable noise.
  We present a 3D visualization approach mapping topics found across a project's socio-technical artifacts onto vegetation-covered islands, where the individual artifacts are depicted as trees of various types. The topic islands rise out of the ocean as they become discussed, to sink again when they are no longer so. We built a prototype implementing the entire visualization pipeline, from data mining to interactive rendering, leveraging machine learning techniques to cluster the artifacts and extract their topics. We present, through several case studies, the insights that our approach elicits about discussions of development topics throughout a project's history. The user study we conducted (N=34) further strengthens our conclusions about its suitability for understanding socio-technical artifacts and their evolution.

</details>


### [30] [Ritgard: T(r)opical Islands of Socio-Technical Artifacts on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05278)
*Adam Štěpánek,Marco Raglianti,Jan Byška,Barbora Kozlíková,Michele Lanza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ritgard 是一个用于挖掘和可视化 GitHub 社会技术制品（Issues、Pull Requests 和 Discussions）的工具，通过主题建模与时间演化展示项目架构、决策和关注点的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 项目中的重要信息不仅存在于源代码，还分散在不同自然语言沟通渠道中，导致架构决策、用户问题和功能演化等信息难以统一提取、浏览和理解。

Method: Ritgard 从 GitHub 仓库收集 Issues、Pull Requests 和 Discussions，结合文本嵌入与文本摘要提取主题，并将每个制品表示为树、将主题表示为岛屿。岛屿地形会随主题活跃度上升或衰退而升起或下沉，以编码项目生命周期中的时间演化。

Result: 论文介绍了 Ritgard 的使用方式与实现，并讨论了构建该可视化系统所面临的多项技术挑战。工具能够以直观的三维形式组织分散的社会技术制品，展示项目中功能与关注点的变化。

Conclusion: Ritgard 为探索项目非代码信息提供了一种结合主题语义、制品组织和时间演化的三维可视化方法，有助于理解项目架构及其演进过程。

Abstract: A software project is more than just code. Non-code artifacts often document the human processes and decisions behind source code. The rationale behind a library change, an architectural decision, a problem encountered by a user are all examples of information typically present in socio-technical artifacts (STAs), created and persisted in channels separate from the repository itself (yet sometimes very close---e.g., GitHub Issues with GitHub repositories). These STAs are a trove of information about the project's architecture and its evolution, containing details and insights that code alone cannot provide. Unfortunately, this information is not easily extracted and explored as STAs are frequently fragmented over different communication channels, and are written in natural language.
  We present Ritgard, a tool that mines GitHub repositories for their STAs, namely Issues, Pull Requests, and Discussions, and visualizes them as 3D islands covered with trees. Each tree represents a single artifact and each island is a topic extracted from the artifacts through a combination of text embedding and text summarization. The terrain of the islands rises out of the ocean as the topic becomes active and sinks back in when it becomes stale, thus depicting the evolution of features and concerns throughout the project's lifetime. We describe the tool's usage and implementation, showing the numerous technical challenges behind Ritgard's visualization.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [31] [Corten - Foundational Verification of Rust Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04372)
*František Farka,Carmine Abate,Sven Linker,Sebastian Ertel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Corten 是一个基于 Rocq 与 Iris 分离逻辑的 Rust 程序验证框架，在 THIR 层形式化 Rust 语义，并提供程序逻辑与自动化证明机制。实验表明其可显著缩小证明规模，并成功验证 buddy allocator 的内存安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Rust 形式化验证通常依赖较低层的编译器内部表示，导致证明目标难以对应源代码、维护成本高。Corten 旨在直接基于 Rust Reference 形式化接近源代码的 Rust 语义，为可维护的端到端软硬件验证建立统一基础。

Method: Corten 将 Rust 的类型化高级中间表示 THIR 深度嵌入 Rocq，并将动态语义形式化为弱前置条件谓词变换演算。在此基础上构建基于 Iris 的程序逻辑，引入反函数化的 continuation stack 以保持证明目标的一阶性和紧凑性，同时提供语法导向的证明自动化。框架通过 interaction-trees 表示逐个语言构造进行增量式正确性证明。

Result: 合成测试表明，相比直接进行语义证明，Corten 可将证明规模降低约 2 至 4 倍。案例研究中，框架成功验证了 buddy allocator 的分配与释放函数的内存安全性。

Conclusion: Corten 首次在证明助手中提供了带有程序逻辑的、基于 Rust 表面语义的机械化验证框架，并通过 THIR 连接形式化语义与源代码。其证明自动化、紧凑的证明表示和已完成的内存安全案例验证，展示了面向可维护 Rust 验证及软硬件统一语义基础的潜力。

Abstract: We present Corten, a foundational verification framework for Rust programs in the Rocq theorem prover, built on the Iris separation logic framework. Corten provides the first semantics of surface-level Rust mechanized in a proof assistant with an attached program logic, directly grounded in the Rust Reference: it deeply embeds the Typed High-level Intermediate Representation (THIR) into Rocq and formalises Rust's dynamic semantics as a weakest-precondition predicate transformer calculus. By operating at THIR rather than on internal compiler representations, Corten proof goals display the THIR AST, which pretty-prints to surface Rust, keeping verification close to the source code and facilitating maintainability as code evolves. Atop this semantics, Corten develops a program logic and a syntax-directed proof automation layer; the program logic includes defunctionalized continuation stacks that keep proof goals first-order and compact. Soundness is established incrementally, construct by construct, against an interaction-trees denotation. A synthetic test suite demonstrates a two-to-four times reduction in proof size compared to raw semantic proofs. We further showcase Corten on a buddy allocator case study, verifying memory safety of the allocation and deallocation functions, laying the groundwork for end-to-end verification in a shared Rocq semantic foundation spanning hardware-software boundaries.

</details>


### [32] [JLIR: A Julia-Native MLIR-Inspired Intermediate Representation with Automatic JACC Kernel Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04585)
*Narasinga Rao Miniskar,Seyong Lee,Keita Teranishi,Jeffrey S Vetter*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出JLIR（Julia-native Level Intermediate Representation），一种原生于Julia的多层次中间表示框架，旨在将MLIR式的多层次、方言化编译能力引入Julia，同时保留Julia代码的自然表达和动态特性，并通过JACC加速器内核生成验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIR的静态类型约束和以C++为中心的扩展方式不适合Julia等高层、动态特化语言，导致领域库中的线性代数、网格处理和偏微分方程等抽象难以进入编译器优化流程。非编译器用户也难以自然地定义新的编程抽象并保持其可优化性，因此需要更贴合Julia语言机制的中间表示框架。

Method: JLIR在Julia程序降至低层表示之前进行建模，将程序表示为可扩展的操作和方言；利用Julia自身的语言机制实现转换操作与优化遍；允许部分类型化程序在具体类型确定前继续进行变换。框架内置算术、控制流、函数、结构化循环和内存操作等方言，并提供无需修改核心系统即可添加领域操作的轻量机制。

Result: JLIR实现了面向Julia的多层次、方言化中间表示基础设施，使中间表示能够作为普通Julia代码使用，并支持可扩展操作、转换流程及延迟类型确定。作者将其应用于JACC自动生成加速器内核，以展示该框架支持领域特定编译和优化的能力。摘要未报告具体性能数据或与其他方案的定量比较。

Conclusion: JLIR为Julia动态语言提供了比传统MLIR扩展模式更自然的编译基础设施，有望缩短领域抽象进入优化编译路径的距离，并降低非编译器专家扩展编译系统的门槛。其当前证据主要是框架设计与JACC案例验证，实际性能收益和扩展性仍需通过更广泛的基准测试进一步评估。

Abstract: The Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) has made reusable compiler infrastructure practical for domain-specific computation. However, MLIR's strong compile-time type requirements and low-level (C++) extension model can be a poor match for high-level, dynamically specialized languages such as Julia. MLIR has several drawbacks for dynamic programming languages in terms of the type system and level of abstraction. It is thus extremely challenging for non-compiler or scientific computing users to introduce new programming abstractions and express algorithm implementations in a form that remains both natural and optimizable. As a result, library interfaces for linear algebra, mesh processing, partial differential equations, and related domains often sit outside the compiler optimization path. We present JLIR (Julia-native Level Intermediate Representation), a Julia-native intermediate representation framework that brings the main benefits of MLIR-style multi-level, dialect-oriented compilation into the Julia ecosystem while remaining usable as ordinary Julia code. JLIR represents Julia programs before low-level lowering, supports extensible operations and transformation passes through Julia's language mechanisms, and allows partially typed programs to remain transformable until concrete types are known. The framework includes built-in dialects for arithmetic, control flow, functions, structured loops, and memory operations, and it also includes a lightweight mechanism for adding new domain operations without modifying the core system. To demonstrate JLIR's capabilities, we applied it to automatic Julia for Accelerators (JACC) kernel generation.

</details>


### [33] [CPL: A Compact C-like Systems Language with Explicit Low-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04904)
*Nikolay Fot,Alexander Vinarsky*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Cordell Programming Language（CPL），一种保持 C 语言底层控制能力、同时简化语法并引入部分现代语言便利特性的紧凑型系统编程语言。实验表明，其原型编译器生成的代码性能可接近 GCC、Clang、TinyC 和 SmallerC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索：相较于现代语言及其编程范式，保留 C 风格内存、布局和机器接口控制能力、但采用更小语法体系的语言，是否更适合编译器实验和系统软件开发。

Method: 论文设计并实现 CPL 及其编译器，支持原始值、指针、数组、容器方法、联合体、泛型函数、重载、入口点与段控制、系统调用和内联汇编。研究同时描述了编译器流水线、目标后端、静态分析架构及面向操作系统的使用场景，并在 x86_64 和 i386 平台上，通过可复现的微基准测试，将原型后端与 GCC、Clang、TinyC 和 SmallerC 的编译结果进行比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，CPL 编译器能够生成性能与生产级编译器 GCC、Clang 以及小型编译器 TinyC、SmallerC 相当的机器代码。论文还表明，CPL 在保留底层控制能力的同时，提供了比传统 C 更精简且具备部分现代特性的语言结构。

Conclusion: CPL 初步验证了小型 C-like 语言用于编译器实验和系统级开发的可行性。其编译结果具有竞争力，但由于缺少类、内置方法、标准库和内存保护等高层机制，当前定位更偏向编译器研究、底层系统开发和操作系统相关场景，而非通用应用开发。

Abstract: This paper presents Cordell Programming Language (CPL), a compact C-like systems language that retains C's direct access to memory, layout, and machine interfaces while experimenting with a smaller grammar and selected conveniences from newer languages. Also this paper studies whether C-like are more convenient to use for compiler experiments than modern approaches and paradigms. While the language and compiler provide primitive values, pointers, arrays, containers with methods, unions, generic functions, overloads, entry-point and section control, system calls, and inline assembly, they do not provide high-level constructs such as classes, built-in methods, a standard library, or memory protection. The article describes the language design, compiler pipeline, target backends, static-analysis architecture, and OS-facing use cases, then evaluates the prototype backend with reproducible x86_64 and i386 microbenchmarks against C compiler baselines. The obtained results suggest that the compiler can produce code comparable to that produced by production compilers such as GCC and Clang, as well as by small compilers such as TinyC and SmallerC.

</details>


### [34] [Augur: Predicting View Serializability Violations in Relational Data Store Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05288)
*Chujun Geng,Noah Charlton,Spyros Blanas,Michael D. Bond,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出Augur，一种面向数据存储应用的动态预测程序分析方法，可处理带复杂谓词的关系型查询，并仅报告违反视图可串行化的可行执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据存储应用常通过弱隔离换取可扩展性能，但这会导致偶发的不可串行化执行，从而产生错误结果或失败。已有基于执行轨迹的动态预测分析无法有效支持复杂SQL谓词，且会预测实际上不违反视图可串行化的执行，造成误报。
