Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [134] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍一个用于检验Transformer语言模型是否能根据上下文区分同形异义词的开放工具包。工具包以跨学科、不同义项的“桥接词”为核心，涵盖数据构建、语料获取、表示提取、层级分析、分离度测量和可视化流程，但不报告具体模型或词集的实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词嵌入层通常为同一词形分配单一且与上下文无关的向量，而后续层是否能可靠地区分其不同语境和词义仍需严格检验。为避免词形、语境和标签混杂，研究需要一种词形固定、领域与预期词义可控且带有明确标注的分析构造。

Method: 定义跨两个或多个学科领域、词形不变但词义不同的桥接词及其来源领域；从Wikipedia获取语料并定位词项出现位置；提取Transformer各层的表示；针对领域对计算表示空间中的分离度，并采用成对可视化进行比较。方法同时处理宽泛类别导致的词义污染、轮廓系数在多组情形下的偏差、子词切分与词项对齐问题，以及降维图坐标不可比等风险。

Result: 本文是方法与实现参考文档，未在特定模型或桥接词集合上运行工具包，也未报告或解释任何实证结果。工具包源码及用于演示的语料另行归档，并提供持久化标识符供后续研究引用。

Conclusion: 该工具包为研究Transformer表示中的上下文词义区分能力提供了可复现、模块化的实验基础。其主要贡献是明确分析构造、完整处理流程及针对常见方法学失效模式的设计规范，而非提出关于模型表现的经验性结论。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [135] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [136] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨学生在明确得知评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成后，如何理解和评价AI介导的评估。结果表明，学生认可AI反馈在表层修改方面的实用性，但不认为AI应拥有最终评分权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较多关注生成式AI反馈能否提升写作表现及其有用性，却较少分析学生在知晓反馈和分数由AI生成后，如何理解其评价效力、可信度及教学意义。

Method: 研究采用定性教学探究方法，在一门面向计算机专业本科生的技术传播课程中开展。13名本科生完成课堂手写写作任务，扫描稿由依据任务目标设计的评分标准提示词驱动的ChatGPT进行评分和反馈；学生获知AI评价来源后，以书面形式进行反思，并通过归纳式主题分析提炼主题。

Result: 分析 выяв出四个主题：反馈的感知有用性；对AI情境理解和教学局限的认识；有条件的信任，即区分反馈效用与评价权威；以及对教师制度性和教学角色的反思。学生认为AI反馈可用于表层修改，但普遍认为评分决策应由人类教师负责。

Conclusion: 学生将“反馈实用性”和“评价权威性”视为两个相互区分的判断维度，而不是单一认可程度的两端。生成式AI可以辅助修订和反馈，但教师仍被视为具有最终评分权和教学判断权的主体。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [137] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个将真实桌面软件转化为可扩展混合GUI+CLI环境的数据与训练管线，旨在提升计算机使用智能体的任务成功率与操作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体主要依赖GUI，操作轨迹冗长低效；CLI智能体又缺乏视觉感知能力，难以处理界面状态和布局相关任务。由于为真实应用同时构建GUI与CLI环境需要大量人工工程，现有可扩展的混合环境较为稀缺，因此需要一种能够统一支持两种交互模态的自动化方案。

Method: CUA-Universe由三个组件构成：App-Forge将应用封装为可复现虚拟机，并发现、封装或生成其命令行接口，覆盖16个应用；Task-Weave基于可复用操作和种子文件，合成具有多样性和可控难度的混合任务；Path-Steer引导智能体沿更高效的GUI+CLI路径执行，并收集经过验证的轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 训练后，模型从低效的纯GUI操作和脆弱的CLI脚本，转向更有效的GUI与CLI协同。在CUA-Verse上，9B模型得分提升39.3个百分点、步骤数减少37%、令牌数减少60%；在OSWorld上，成功率提升16.8个百分点、步骤数减少57%、令牌数减少44%；在OSWorld-MCP上，得分提升7.84个百分点、步骤数减少27%、令牌数减少30%。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe证明了通过自动化环境构建、混合任务合成和高效轨迹采集，可以规模化训练更具执行能力和效率的计算机使用智能体。其核心贡献在于实现真实桌面应用上的GUI与CLI协同，并在多个基准上同时改善任务成功率与交互成本。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [138] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文审计了22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子性质回归基准上的逐字检索现象，指出常规准确率无法区分模型真正预测性质与直接复现已发表数值；检索在部分数据集上十分普遍，并会显著受推理等级影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质基准常被用于衡量LLM的科学预测能力，但若训练语料包含基准分子及其标签，模型可能通过记忆和检索获得高分，从而夸大其泛化预测能力。论文旨在量化该混淆因素，并考察推理设置与抑制检索措施的作用。

Method: 作者在12个回归数据集上测试22个模型，检测其是否输出与公开标签完全一致的数值，以识别逐字检索；实验分别在两种推理等级下进行，并比较相同分子、提示词条件下的检索触发差异。对于检索污染最明显的案例，作者通过变换SMILES表示同时保留原始标签来尝试中断检索，并分析抑制前后各模型误差的相对分布。

Result: 在5个数据集中，超过50%的受测模型表现出逐字检索；其余数据集则主要仅出现零星模型—数据集组合。较高推理等级下，同一实验条件被标记为检索的频率比最低推理等级高89%。即使使用变换后的SMILES，部分强模型仍可将其与原始标签关联。抑制检索后，不同模型的相对预测误差更趋近；而模型对逐字检索的不同依赖会拉大其表现差距。

Conclusion: 分子回归基准上的LLM性能不能仅依据准确率解读，必须控制或审计训练数据记忆导致的逐字检索。检索程度具有显著的数据集特异性，并受推理配置影响；模型的基础预测能力也并非单纯由记忆标签数量决定。论文为评估分子性质预测中的数据污染、记忆与真实泛化提供了系统证据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [139] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究检验大语言模型所引用的决策因素，是否真正对应其可观测行为中的关键影响因素。结果显示，引用因素具有一定信息价值，但无法稳定、准确地识别影响输出最强的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体工作流中，操作人员可能依据模型解释进行系统监控、错误诊断或升级决策，因此解释应与模型实际决策行为一致。研究重点考察解释因素的必要性与充分性是否成立。

Method: 研究在 advisor 推荐和提示词风险判断两个合成场景中评估八个来自 Claude、GPT 和 Gemini 系列的模型。模型需输出结果及其认为影响最大的三个因素。通过黑箱干预分别计算必要性分数（改变某因素后输出发生变化的频率）和充分性分数（保留该因素并移除其他可变信息后输出保持不变的频率），再与模型给出的因素排名进行比较。

Result: 在 advisor 推荐场景中，引用排名与必要性、充分性分数的平均 Spearman 相关分别为0.349和0.354；在提示词监测场景中分别为0.431和0.580。未被引用因素的得分超过最低引用因素的比例，在 advisor 场景中必要性和充分性分别为57.6%和58.1%，在提示词监测场景中分别为25.8%和8.9%。

Conclusion: 模型列出的前三个因素包含部分有用信息，但不能可靠代表必要性或充分性意义下影响最强的因素。该黑箱评估框架可用于检验单次LLM决策解释的可靠性，为智能体监督提供辅助检查，但不等同于完整的因果解释或全局模型解释。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出KOPA-Bench和EDGE，用真实公共API执行结果构建可验证的多步工具调用训练数据，并通过GRPO微调显著提升开源模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源大语言模型在需要连续调用多个工具、跨API传递结果的复杂任务上表现不足，且缺少能够衡量这一差距的基准。研究旨在建立真实、可执行的评测与数据合成方法。

Method: 构建包含145项真实任务的KOPA-Bench。提出EDGE方法，通过实际调用在线API发现工具输出与后续工具输入之间的关系，仅保留执行成功的连接，再沿经验证的图结构生成可执行多步轨迹。随后使用这些数据通过GRPO对9B模型进行微调。

Result: 微调后的9B模型在KOPA-Bench上的表现大幅提升，接近同系列未经微调的27B模型；同时在BFCL基准上也取得明显改进，表明方法具有一定跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于真实执行反馈构建工具依赖图和训练轨迹，能够有效提升开源模型的多步工具调用能力。EDGE与KOPA-Bench为公共API场景下的可执行数据合成和系统化评测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [141] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度学习的Inter-Instance生成对抗网络（IIns-GAN），用于生成具有位置标签且符合真实物理特征的无线信号，以降低真实数据采集与标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知模型依赖带有位置相关标签的信号数据，但真实数据集采集和标注成本高。传统基于环境模型的信号合成方法需要大量超参数调节，且生成信号的真实性和泛化能力不足，难以支持多样化模型训练任务。

Method: 构建IIns-GAN，通过生成对抗学习生成带标签的无线信号，并增强其对不同环境场景的适应性。该方法面向距离估计、环境识别等无线感知任务进行数据生成与训练支持，并在公开超宽带（UWB）数据集上开展实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，IIns-GAN生成的信号能够较好地复现真实测量信号的物理特征；生成数据还能够有效提升距离估计和环境识别等无线感知任务的模型训练效果。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN为低成本构建真实感较高的带标签无线信号数据提供了有效方案，在不同环境和多种无线感知任务中具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Blockchain-Enabled Secure Logging for Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms: Evaluation of the Greek eSEND and myDATA Tax Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04356)
*Panagiotis Mavridis,Anargyros Baklezos,Christos Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文比较了希腊财务电子设备与电子发票平台的完整性保障机制，指出采用硬件设备、哈希链和顺序校验的财务设备比纯软件化电子发票架构具有更强的防篡改与交易完整性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化税务系统需要确保交易数据不可篡改、完整且可追溯。论文旨在评估现有财务设备及电子发票基础设施的密码学完整性设计，识别软件化电子发票体系中的架构缺口。

Method: 研究分析多类财务电子设备的密码学架构，包括电子收银机、财务打印机、财务签名机和FEMAS设备，考察其双重或三重哈希链设计；同时评估设备至中央税务数据库的传输协议，重点关注加密、顺序验证与区块链验证。随后将其与电子发票服务提供商及myDATA中央平台的架构进行对比。

Result: 硬件财务设备通过哈希链、加密传输、交易序列验证和区块链式校验，能够较有效地保证交易记录的连续性、完整性和抗篡改性。相比之下，电子发票服务与myDATA平台缺少等效的区块链完整性保障机制，因此其对交易完整性和完备性的保护相对较弱。

Conclusion: 论文认为，现有电子发票框架存在完整性保障不足的问题。为提升数字税务生态系统的可信性，应借鉴硬件财务设备的密码学与链式验证设计，在电子发票平台中引入更强的不可篡改记录、顺序一致性验证及端到端完整性控制机制。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the implementation of blockchain-based integrity mechanisms in Greek Fiscal Electronic Mechanisms (FEMs) and the central tax information system eSEND. The study examines the cryptographic architecture of fiscal devices, including Electronic Cash Registers, Fiscal Printers, Fiscal Signing Machines, and FEMAS devices, which implement double or triple hash-chain structures to ensure transaction immutability. The transmission protocol between fiscal devices and the central database is also evaluated with respect to encryption, sequential validation, and blockchain verification. In contrast, the architecture of Electronic Invoicing Provider Services and the myDATA central platform is analyzed, highlighting the absence of blockchain-based integrity guarantees. The comparison demonstrates that hardware-based fiscal mechanisms provide stronger guarantees for transaction completeness and tamper resistance than purely software-based invoicing infrastructures. The findings highlight architectural weaknesses in the current e-invoicing framework and propose improvements for ensuring transaction integrity in digital tax ecosystems.

</details>


### [143] [Privacy Failure in Split-LLM Training, The Returned Gradient Nullifies the Decoys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04382)
*Georgios Politis,Evangelos Pappas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究发现，一种两节点分割式大语言模型训练系统虽然通过了前向通道隐私评估，但返回梯度中的零值模式泄露了真实数据行的位置。跨九个随机种子，系统在全部4096个样本帧中准确识别真实行；进一步攻击使每百个样本多恢复约一个token。对梯度逐行裁剪并加噪可在约0.01 nats的交叉熵代价下阻断该泄露，但系统仍未覆盖五类潜在攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私评估主要关注受保护激活或前向通信，可能忽略训练协议中返回梯度产生的侧信道。研究旨在验证：当云端接收真实行与诱饵行的混合输入、而诱饵行损失为零时，梯度中的零模式是否会暴露真实数据位置，以及这种问题是否存在于具有实际部署质量约束的配置中。

Method: 构建两节点分割式训练协议：可信本地节点持有私有损失并返回输出梯度，非可信云节点处理混合帧。预先固定评估协议，包括已知强度的注入泄露、打乱标签的阴性对照和预设检测阈值。使用九个随机种子统计零梯度模式对真实行的识别率，并测试基于帧内容的恢复攻击；随后在满足模型质量预算的配置和两个数据集上复现实验。最后评估逐行梯度裁剪与加噪的缓解效果。

Result: 零值模式在每个实验帧中都正确识别真实行，九次运行共识别4096/4096。内容恢复攻击相对恒定猜测基线额外恢复约0.65至1.50个百分点，即每百个样本约多恢复一个token；打乱标签对照未产生恢复效果。两个数据集上的相关运行均通过前向通道隐私检查和质量检查，但纳入返回梯度后均检测到泄露。逐行裁剪和加噪可关闭该泄露，代价约为0.01 nats的留出交叉熵。

Conclusion: 隐私评估必须覆盖完整训练闭环，而不能仅检查前向激活或通信内容；梯度稀疏性和零模式本身可能构成高可靠侧信道。该研究证明了可观测性泄露及一种有效缓解方式，但不能据此宣称系统整体安全，因为跨训练步骤累积观测等五类攻击尚未评估。

Abstract: We present a systems-security case study of a two-node split-LLM training system whose privacy evaluation passed while leaving an observable channel untested. The Trusted Local Node (TLN) sends protected activations to the Untrusted Cloud Node (UCN), the UCN returns its output, and TLN, holding the private loss, returns the output gradient. The frame the UCN receives mixes real rows with decoys, and the loss ignores the decoys. Their gradients are exactly zero, so the pattern of zeros reveals which rows were real. We measure it with a protocol fixed in advance: a leak injected at known strength to prove the instrument can see one, a shuffled-label control to prove it does not report absent leaks, and a threshold set before the runs. Across nine seeds, the zeros identified the real rows on every frame, 4,096 of 4,096 per run. An attack on the frame contents recovered about one extra token per hundred over a constant-guess baseline (+0.65 to +1.50 percentage points); the shuffled controls recovered nothing. A second set of runs repeated this on a configuration that keeps model quality within budget, so the finding is not confined to a setting nobody would deploy. On both datasets, every such run passed the forward-channel privacy check and the quality check, yet failed that same check once the returned gradient was included. Clipping and noising each row of the gradient closed the leak for about 0.01 nats of held-out cross-entropy. The system is not thereby safe: five classes of attack, including those accumulating observations across training steps, were never measured.

</details>


### [144] [Candidate Comparability Before Promotion: Conditional Validation in Adaptive Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04388)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，入侵检测模型的更新是否应替换现有模型，不能仅依据漂移告警判断，还显著受挑战者模型构建方式及其训练证据量影响。采用独立的挑战者预处理流程并增加候选样本后，更新效果总体改善，但不同数据集与策略之间不存在普遍最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应入侵检测通常在检测到概念漂移后直接重训或部署新模型，但漂移存在并不等于新模型优于当前模型。模型晋升会改变后续检测机制，因此需要区分“发现变化”与“证明替代合理”这两个问题，并控制挑战者构建和评估证据带来的方法学偏差。

Method: 作者在CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15和ToN-IoT三个基准上比较九种更新策略。实验使用独立封装的挑战者训练流水线、嵌套式候选样本规模控制和统一评测框架，并进行最终敏感性分析，使每个精确特征向量仅承担评估、训练或探测中的一种角色。研究进一步比较不同候选证据规模、候选模型可比性、验证机制以及真实时间序列流量回放下的部署结果。

Result: 当沿用由现有模型控制且冻结的预处理时，模型晋升的负面影响被明显放大；采用自包含挑战者流水线后，平均的完整漂移损害不再持续。将每类候选样本从512增加至2000后，在渐进漂移条件下，三个基准的平衡准确率分别提高0.53、1.67和0.38个百分点，且均具有统计显著性，但提升幅度存在明显数据集差异，主要来自误报减少。策略排序会随挑战者可比性改变，没有任何策略在所有场景中占优。验证主要帮助证据不足的挑战者，在证据对等时平均无额外收益。对13段真实时间顺序流量的回放显示，始终部署挑战者未产生净损害。

Conclusion: 模型晋升评估应明确控制、报告并解释挑战者的构建方式和证据规模。漂移告警本身不足以支持模型替换决策；独立且可比的挑战者流水线、充分的候选数据以及严格的数据角色隔离，是获得可靠更新结论的关键。实际系统不应假设某一更新策略具有全局最优性，而应结合具体数据集、候选证据充足度和误报代价进行选择。

Abstract: Adaptive network intrusion detection systems retrain classifiers after drift alarms, but an alarm detects change; it does not establish that a challenger should replace the deployed incumbent. Promotion is security-relevant because it changes the model responsible for subsequent attack detection, and evaluating it has a methodological problem: promotion conclusions may depend on how the challenger was constructed and on how much evidence supports it. We test that dependence on CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15 and ToN-IoT with self-contained challenger pipelines, nested candidate-size controls, a common-harness comparison of nine update policies, and a final sensitivity confining every exact feature vector to one evaluation, training or probe role. Incumbent-owned frozen preprocessing amplified apparent promotion harm; with self-contained challenger pipelines the mean full-drift harm did not persist. Raising nominal candidate evidence from 512 to 2,000 samples per class improved promotion under pool-constructed progressive drift by +0.53, +1.67 and +0.38 balanced-accuracy points: positive and statistically resolved in all three benchmarks, but materially benchmark-dependent rather than homogeneous, and driven mainly by fewer false positives. Policy conclusions were partially robust: policy ordering changed with candidate comparability, no policy globally dominated, and earlier compatibility statements for a label-free estimator and a calibrated ensemble narrowed. Validation helped evidence-disadvantaged challengers but added no average benefit at parity. Thirteen replays on real, time-ordered traffic showed no net harm from always deploying. Challenger construction and evidence should be controlled, reported and interpreted explicitly when promotion is evaluated.

</details>


### [145] [Engineered Persuasion: Evaluating Personalized Pretexts in LLM-Generated Spear Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04410)
*Jerson Francia,Derek Hansen,Benjamin Schooley,Shydra Valynn Murray*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究表明，AI生成钓鱼邮件中的个性化信息总体上会提升可信度和点击意图，但效果取决于细节是否符合收件人的工作情境；不准确、模糊或不合适的沟通渠道信息反而会引发怀疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型降低了生成带有职场细节的钓鱼诱饵的成本，但个性化信息究竟增强还是削弱邮件可信度仍不明确。本研究旨在检验不同层级职场信息对说服力、点击意图及其他安全行为的影响，并识别细节发挥作用的条件。

Method: 研究招募180名美国在职成年人，在明确告知为调查的条件下评估AI生成的模拟钓鱼邮件。邮件包含四个累积信息层级：工作场所、收件人姓名与职位、工作职责、同事或共享项目背景。参与者对邮件可信度进行0至100分评价，选择打开链接、调查、删除或报告中的一项行动，并解释最高分与最低分邮件的原因。研究共获得1436次有效评价，并结合敏感性分析、描述性分析和定性编码进行评估。

Result: 敏感性分析显示，可信度平均随个性化层级增加而提高，每增加一级约提升2.40分；表达点击意图的优势比每级增加28%。在未表示点击意图的参与者中，调查行为仍较常见，报告行为减少，删除行为增加。描述性分析显示，相比来自部门或实体的邮件，由具名人员发送且提及指定同事的邮件获得更高评分和更强点击意图。定性结果表明，与个人职责和日常工作相符的细节增强可信度，而错误、模糊或不符合沟通渠道的细节会提高怀疑。

Conclusion: 职场钓鱼邮件的个性化并非简单地增加信息数量即可奏效，其影响关键取决于信息与收件人工作情境的匹配程度。网络安全培训应强调核验细节、发送者身份、沟通渠道和情境合理性，而不应仅依赖邮件是否包含个性化内容来判断风险。

Abstract: Large language models can insert workplace details into phishing pretexts at low cost, but those details may either support or undermine a message's credibility. We recruited 180 U.S. working adults to evaluate simulated, AI-generated phishing emails in a disclosed survey. The emails used four cumulative levels of information: workplace (Level 1); recipient name and job title; job responsibilities; and coworker/shared-project context (Level 4). Participants rated each message's convincingness from 0 to 100, chose one stated action (open the link, investigate, delete, or report), and explained why their highest- and lowest-rated messages stood out. Across 1,436 valid evaluations, convincingness increased by 2.40 points per personalization level in a sensitivity analysis, while the odds of expressing click intention increased by 28\% per level. Among participants who did not express an intention to click, investigation remained common, reporting declined, and deletion increased. A post-hoc descriptive analysis found higher ratings and click intention for messages from a named person who referenced a supplied coworker than for messages from a department or entity. Qualitative coding showed why added detail could help or hurt: details that matched participants' roles and routines supported credibility, while incorrect, vague, or channel-inappropriate details raised suspicion. Together, the results highlight that personalization is not simply a matter of adding more details: it depends on whether the pretext fits the recipient's work context. We discuss how this distinction can inform workplace cybersecurity training.

</details>


### [146] [Nebulon Enterprise Simulated Threats for Phishing Research (NEST-Phish): A Synthetic Enterprise Phishing Email Dataset for Behavioral and Machine-Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04474)
*Emily J. Winokur,Lauren S. Treiman,Allen G. Moore,Paul Schutte,Joe Ingram,Danielle N. Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并公开发布了一个面向企业邮件场景的合成钓鱼邮件数据集，以虚构组织 Nebulon 为背景，包含带有可解释钓鱼线索标注的合法邮件与钓鱼邮件，用于支持检测、用户易感性、可解释性和基准评测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实企业环境中的钓鱼邮件数据通常受隐私、保密和共享限制，公开可用且贴近工作场景的数据集不足。这限制了对钓鱼识别模型、人工判断差异及可解释检测方法的系统研究。

Method: 作者构建了以多种职场沟通主题为覆盖范围的合成邮件语料，设计相互匹配的非钓鱼与钓鱼邮件样本，并为钓鱼样本提供可解释的线索标注。随后通过受试者分类实验评估人类对邮件的判断差异，并通过分类器实验检验数据集中是否存在可供监督学习利用的检测信号。

Result: 实验表明，该数据集能够引发具有实际意义的人类钓鱼判断差异，说明其样本并非过于简单或同质；同时，分类器评估显示数据集包含可学习的区分信号，能够支持监督式钓鱼检测任务。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了公开、企业情境化钓鱼邮件数据资源不足的问题。其主要贡献是提供具有主题多样性、配对样本设计和可解释线索标注的合成基准资源；但由于数据完全合成，其与真实组织邮件分布及真实攻击策略之间的外部有效性仍需在后续研究中进一步验证。

Abstract: Phishing remains one of the most persistent cyber threats, yet publicly shareable datasets for studying phishing in realistic enterprise email settings remain limited. To address this gap, we introduce a synthetic enterprise phishing email dataset built around a fictitious organization, Nebulon. The dataset spans a broad set of workplace communication themes and includes matched synthetic legitimate and phishing emails with interpretable phishing-cue annotations. Here, ``legitimate'' denotes the non-phishing class, not legitimately occurring organizational emails. Human-subject categorizations and classifier evaluations show that the dataset supports meaningful variation in phishing judgments while also providing learnable signal for supervised detection. This publicly released resource is intended to support future work on phishing detection, human susceptibility, explainability, and benchmark development in enterprise-like contexts.

</details>


### [147] [Client-Side Probing of Deleted Ridge Statistics in Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04475)
*Yijun Quan,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一类联邦遗忘系统的隐私与完整性风险：服务器广播更新后的线性分类器可能泄露被删除样本、类别或客户端摘要，甚至允许攻击者重新插入已删除信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效联邦遗忘方法依赖紧凑的可加训练摘要，并在每次变更后广播更新模型。研究动机是检验这些广播是否会暴露服务器内部状态，以及删除操作是否真正保护了客户端数据。

Method: 作者建立代数分析框架，刻画观测结果何时包含足够的独立信息；针对不受限探测构造最优攻击，并提出仅利用攻击者自身数据形成增量的更现实估计方法。实验在MNIST和CIFAR-10上开展，同时考察广播精度、探测多样性、更新规模、验证机制、响应频率和并发活动的影响。

Result: 在高精度广播下，两类探测方法均能对测试中的每个样本删除实现精确标签恢复。降低广播精度会显著削弱细粒度恢复；响应缺乏足够多样性时则无法完成状态识别。不受限探测因规模异常而容易被发现，而基于攻击者自身数据的单次增量通常类似正常批次，但完整攻击序列是否隐蔽尚未得到保证。

Conclusion: 联邦遗忘中的模型广播可能构成可操作的隐私泄露和完整性攻击面，其根本原因是广播响应携带了关于可加内部摘要的线性信息。系统应限制广播精度和响应频率，验证更新来源与结构，并引入并发或其他机制降低状态可识别性；仅执行数据删除而不保护更新通道，不能确保遗忘有效。

Abstract: Federated unlearning aims to remove a client's data from a shared model without retraining from scratch. Some efficient systems make deletion exact by storing compact, additive summaries of the training features and broadcasting an updated linear classifier after every accepted change. We show that these broadcasts can also reveal the hidden summaries. A malicious client can submit known changes, use the returned classifiers to identify the server state, and compare states immediately before and after an isolated deletion. This exposes the deleted sample, class, or client summary and can enable its reinsertion. We characterize exactly when the observations contain enough independent information, give a matching optimal construction for unrestricted probes, and derive a more realistic estimator based on additions formed from the attacker's own data. On MNIST and CIFAR-10, high-precision broadcasts permit exact label recovery for every tested sample deletion with both probe types. Lower-precision broadcasts sharply reduce fine-grained recovery, and insufficiently diverse responses prevent identification altogether. Unrestricted probes are readily detected by their size; most individual attacker-data additions resemble honest batches, although we do not claim that the complete sequence is inconspicuous. The results identify a concrete privacy and integrity risk, its algebraic cause, and practical limits involving broadcast precision, update verification, response rate, and concurrent activity.

</details>


### [148] [Hoss: Fast Oblivious Semantic Search with Heterogeneous GPU-CPU-TEE Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04522)
*Jianzhang Du,Weijie Huang,Chenghong Wang,Nicolas Tsagareli,Yukui Luo,XiaoFeng Wang,Zhongshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Hoss利用GPU TEE的大容量私有内存，将HNSW语义搜索的热点遍历放入GPU侧，仅对超出容量的低层图数据调用CPU TEE中的混淆访问机制，从而在保持高召回率的同时显著降低开销，最高实现67倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统Compass基于ORAM保护语义搜索中的数据内容和访问模式，但ORAM访问受带宽下界限制，成为主要性能瓶颈。现代GPU TEE具备较大的私有内存，可隐藏内部访问模式，为减少传统ORAM开销提供了新的系统设计空间。

Method: Hoss采用异构CPU-GPU TEE架构：将HNSW图的热点、高频遍历路径放入GPU TEE私有内存；当图的低层数据超过GPU容量时，将其卸载至CPU TEE，并仅在访问这些低层数据时使用混淆访问原语。此外，系统设计了面向主机访问的ORAM机制，结合传统设计难以实现的数据相关优化。

Result: 原型系统评测表明，相比Compass，Hoss最高可获得67倍加速，同时保持较高召回率；随着数据规模扩大，性能收益更加明显。

Conclusion: 通过利用GPU TEE的大容量私有内存，将大部分搜索路径从ORAM中移出，Hoss有效缓解了混淆语义搜索的主要性能瓶颈，证明了CPU-GPU异构TEE架构在实现高效、可扩展且低成本隐私保护语义搜索方面的潜力。

Abstract: Semantic search is widely deployed in modern AI systems, but protecting both data contents and access patterns remains challenging. The current state-of-the-art system, Compass, achieves oblivious semantic search by building an optimized ORAM over HNSW graphs. However, even with aggressive optimizations, it still incurs large overheads. Closing this performance gap is fundamentally difficult: Compass has already removed most cryptographic overheads, leaving ORAM accesses as the dominant cost, which are constrained by well-known Omega(log N) bandwidth lower bounds. Our key insight is that traditional ORAM overhead stems from the assumption of limited private memory, whereas modern GPU TEEs provide large private memory (Pmem) that blinds internal access patterns (Hunt et al., NSDI '23). This shift opens a new design space. We therefore propose Hoss, a first-of-its-kind oblivious semantic search system with a heterogeneous CPU-GPU TEE architecture that supports fast, scalable search with low cost of ownership. In Hoss, the GPU TEE's large Pmem hosts the hot-path HNSW traversal, while the lower layers of the graph, if they exceed GPU capacity, are offloaded to CPU TEEs. The system invokes oblivious primitives only when accessing these lower layers. The availability of large Pmem also enables new optimization opportunities. For example, Hoss features a host-access ORAM mechanism that goes beyond traditional performance constraints and incorporates several data-dependent optimizations that are not possible in prior designs. We implement a prototype of Hoss and benchmark it against Compass. Our results show that Hoss achieves up to 67x speedup while maintaining high recall, with larger gains at scale.

</details>


### [149] [Repeat-After-Me: Black-Box Adaptive Visual Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04533)
*Sizhe Chen,Yu-Lin Tsai,Ivan Evtimov,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner,Arman Zharmagambetov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种黑盒自适应视觉提示注入攻击方法 Repeat-After-Me，可在视觉语言模型处理不受信任图像时诱导其输出敏感信息或执行未授权工具调用，并在多类模型与真实代理环境中表现出较高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本提示注入通常具有很高的攻击成功率，但现有视觉提示注入难以生成长文本或严格格式化的目标输出，尤其难以诱导模型产生可解析的工具调用。研究旨在弥补视觉域攻击效果不足，并评估其对实际多模态代理系统的安全影响。

Method: 采用黑盒自适应优化策略，通过构造和迭代视觉注入内容，使模型重复生成攻击者指定的目标字符串或结构化输出。研究在开放权重模型和商业前沿视觉语言模型上进行测试，并评估替代模型迁移、跨样本迁移以及真实代理部署中的攻击效果，同时比较其与自适应文本注入的差异。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-27B等开放权重模型上攻击成功率超过80%，在GPT-5.5等商业模型上超过47%。针对一个替代模型优化的注入，在两个商业受害模型上仍保留43%至46%的原始成功率；跨样本迁移保留64%至66%的原始成功率。在OpenClaw代理的默认部署场景中，最小化图像注入可篡改工具配置文件，并进一步造成高风险代理行为。该攻击还覆盖了部分自适应文本注入无法奏效的场景。

Conclusion: Repeat-After-Me表明，视觉提示注入已能够对具备工具调用能力的多模态代理构成现实安全威胁，且具有一定的跨模型和跨样本迁移能力。研究强调应将图像输入视为不可信数据，强化指令与数据隔离、工具调用授权、配置文件完整性保护、输出格式校验和代理权限控制，并进一步研究针对自适应视觉攻击的防御机制。

Abstract: Prompt injection is widely recognized as a major security threat to AI agents that interact with untrusted external data, such as websites, documents, and emails. Prior work has shown that, in the text domain, black-box prompt injection can achieve near-perfect attack success rates (ASRs). In the image domain, however, existing visual prompt injection methods are substantially less effective in attacking frontier commercial VLMs for materially harmful behavior. Achieving such outputs is hard because it requires a long and/or format-compliant target string, such as a precise, parseable native tool call with exact function names and arguments. We present Repeat-After-Me, a black-box adaptive visual prompt injection attack that can reveal personally identifiable information or make malicious tool calls. Across both open-weight and commercial frontier VLMs, including Qwen3.6-27B and GPT-5.5, our method achieves ASRs exceeding 80% and 47%, respectively, under a realistic setting in which the benign user prompt is semantically unrelated to the injected task and does not verbally authorize it. In our evaluation, injections optimized on one surrogate retain 43-46% of the original ASR on two commercial victims, and cross-sample transferability retains 64-66% of the original ASR on those two models. We test our attack in a real-world OpenClaw agent: in a default OpenClaw Discord deployment, an untrusted user can use a minimally injected image to overwrite TOOLS.md, enabling future sensitive behaviors like remote code execution and secret exfiltration. We show our new attack vector works in cases where adaptive textual prompt injection fails. We discuss potential defenses.

</details>


### [150] [Optimizing Credential Blast Radius Through Trust Boundaries and Delegation Under Post-Quantum Authentication Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04566)
*Pauli Taipale,Harri Lainio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文联合优化信任域划分与凭证派生结构，在满足策略和延迟约束的同时降低凭证泄露后的影响范围；实验表明，联合设计通常优于先划分边界再设计凭证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务划分到相互独立的信任域可限制签发者被攻破后的影响范围，但会增加跨域调用；后量子认证和密钥建立机制又可能在受限或高丢包链路上显著增加延迟。因此需要同时权衡安全风险、凭证传播范围与通信成本。

Method: 构建服务交互图与凭证派生图，并通过信任域分配将二者关联。以加权服务影响定义凭证泄露影响范围，使用线性上界进行优化，并以联合事件模型计算精确期望影响。针对共享签发者分析上界的精确条件；一般问题为NP难，而标量化的双域直接签发情形可化为加权最小割问题。实验采用轨迹回放、实测后量子开销、合成风险输入、固定派生结构及1至6个信任域进行评估。

Result: 在230组穷举合成比较中，联合优化有195组取得低于先划分信任边界方案的影响范围，且在链式委派场景下优势更明显。在延迟预算内，相较单一信任域，最优设计最多可将期望影响降低36%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将风险假设、服务依赖关系和实测跨域成本统一转化为候选信任域及凭证派生方案。联合进行边界与凭证结构设计比顺序决策更有效，但复杂场景需要显式建模凭证传播，以避免线性上界低估风险。

Abstract: Partitioning interacting services into independently rooted trust domains limits issuer-compromise reach while increasing calls across trust boundaries. Post-quantum replacements for public-key authentication and key-establishment mechanisms can increase crossing latency on constrained or lossy paths. We formulate the joint selection of trust domains and credential-derivation structures under policy and latency constraints, linking separate service-interaction and credential-derivation graphs through domain assignment. Credential blast radius measures weighted service impact after compromise. A linear upper bound supports optimization, while a joint event model gives exact expected impact. For shared issuers, the bound is exact under nonoverlapping credential reach and otherwise requires explicit propagation. While the general problem is NP-hard, scalarized two-domain direct issuance reduces to a weighted minimum cut. Joint optimization yields lower blast radius than choosing boundaries first in 195 of 230 exhaustive synthetic comparisons, especially under chained delegation. A trace-derived replay used measured post-quantum costs, synthetic risk inputs, a fixed derivation family, and one to six trust domains. The best design found reduced expected impact by up to 36% relative to one domain within the latency budget. The framework turns risk assumptions and measured crossing costs into candidate trust-domain and credential-derivation designs.

</details>


### [151] [Why Is SHAP Not a Reliable Standalone Explanation Framework for Malware Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04626)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统质疑SHAP在恶意软件检测解释中的可靠性，指出其严格保证只适用于预先定义的特征合作博弈，并不等同于解释恶意软件行为。由于静态PE特征存在强依赖，条件SHAP和干预SHAP都可能产生误导性归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件分析人员需要判断模型是否学习了真实的恶意行为，而非数据集、编译器、加壳方式或恶意软件家族等特定模式。现有SHAP的局部准确性、缺失性和一致性等性质，无法保证解释结果具有行为语义或符合真实可执行文件分布。

Method: 论文从理论和实证两方面分析SHAP。理论上考察特征玩家选择、缺失特征处理规则、背景分布和简化输入映射对解释结果的影响，并分析静态PE特征之间的结构性依赖。重点比较条件SHAP与干预SHAP：前者在冗余特征下的归因分散和错误归因，后者生成偏离真实数据流形的特征组合。实证上使用EMBER-2018、EMBER-2024和BODMAS数据集，在固定的LightGBM与XGBoost检测器上验证这些现象。

Result: 理论证明表明，在具有依赖关系的特征中，条件SHAP可能将模型对某一特征的贡献按约1/m的比例分散到m−1个冗余特征，并把重要性分配给模型未使用的特征；当数据分布变化时，甚至可能反转未使用特征的归因符号。干预SHAP则会评估现实中不存在的特征组合。多个数据集和模型的实验确认了上述归因失真。

Conclusion: SHAP应被视为依赖明确数据分布假设和领域验证的有限诊断工具，而不是恶意软件行为的独立解释方法。可靠使用时必须明确特征分组、背景分布、缺失规则和输入映射，并结合PE结构与恶意软件领域知识验证解释结果。

Abstract: Machine learning is widely used for malware detection, but its decisions must be explained. An analyst needs to know whether a model has learned genuine malicious behavior or only dataset-specific patterns \cite{gaur2021semantics}. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) is the standard tool for this, backed by formal properties such as local accuracy, missingness, and consistency. We argue that these guarantees are insufficient for reliable malware interpretation. We claim SHAP explains a chosen feature-coalition game, not malware behavior in the data. That game is fixed only after the analyst selects the feature players, the missing feature rule, the background distribution, and the simplified input mapping. In static Portable Executable feature spaces, groups such as byte histograms, byte-entropy, strings, headers, sections, imports, and data-directories are not independent signals but are jointly shaped by file structure, packing, compiler behavior, and family conventions. We prove that this dependence makes conditional SHAP dilute a model's feature credit by a factor of $1/m$ across $m-1$ redundant features, attributes importance to features the model never uses, and even reverses the sign of an unused feature's attribution when the data distribution changes; interventional SHAP, meanwhile, queries off-manifold coalitions that no real executable would exhibit. Experiments on EMBER-2018, EMBER-2024, and BODMAS with fixed LightGBM and XGBoost detectors confirm these effects. We therefore position SHAP as a limited diagnostic that requires an explicitly stated data distribution and domain validation, not a standalone account of malware behavior.

</details>


### [152] [Memory-Efficient Designs for Word-Wise Universal Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04769)
*Ardhi Wiratama Baskara Yudha,Erwin Eko Wahyudi,Rian Adam Rajagede,Qian Lou,Yan Solihin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BXT是一个面向全同态加密（FHE）的内存优化框架，通过压缩、序列化、延迟生成和数字裁剪缓解FHE数据膨胀造成的内存瓶颈；在CNN推理中，BXT-CSO50相较100×GPU基线最高实现3.8倍加速，精度损失低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE能够在密文上直接执行计算并保持数据隐私，但其密文数据规模可膨胀数个数量级。随着计算加速，系统瓶颈由算力转向内存，低算术强度进一步限制了整体性能，因此需要降低密文存储、传输和生成开销。

Method: BXT包含四项技术：1）密文压缩，通过种子在运行时重新生成密文组件；2）密文序列化，将系数打包为位数组，并在L2缓存到L1缓存传输期间解包；3）延迟种子生成，将计算密集型伪随机数生成离线工作推迟并分摊到聚合操作中；4）基于故障感知训练的密文数字裁剪，针对Universal FHE调整精度与容错性。

Result: 在CNN推理实验中，BXT-CSO50相较100×GPU基线最高获得3.8倍加速；在比较精度为50%时，模型准确率损失低于1%。摘要未给出不同技术组件的独立贡献、硬件配置、数据集及完整性能对比。

Conclusion: BXT通过协同降低密文容量、内存传输量、随机数生成开销和表示精度，有效缓解了FHE工作负载的内存瓶颈。结果表明，在可控精度损失下，该框架能够显著提升CNN密文推理性能，但其泛化性和各优化组件的独立效果仍需更多实验验证。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, preserving privacy throughout analysis. While its privacy is very strong, FHE is much slower to execute than the original computation. In particular, due to the recent success in accelerating its compute, the performance bottleneck shifts to the memory, especially considering that FHE magnifies the data size by orders of magnitude, resulting in a low arithmetic intensity.
  We propose BXT, an FHE optimization framework that mitigates the memory bottleneck through four techniques: (1) ciphertext compression, which regenerates ciphertext components from seeds during execution; (2) ciphertext serialization, which packs coefficients as bit arrays and unpacks them during L2-to-L1 transfer; (3) delayed seed generation, which defers PRNG-heavy offline work across aggregated operations; and (4) ciphertext digit pruning guided by fault-aware training tailored for Universal FHE. On CNN inference, the BXT-CSO50 configuration effectively achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over the 100x GPU baseline with less than 1% accuracy loss at 50% comparison precision.

</details>


### [153] [Injected and Leaked: Actively Inducing Side-Channel Leakage Using Electromagnetic Injection and Hardware Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04785)
*Haoran Yan,Ziyu Shao,Shuhao Zhang,Qinhong Jiang,Yan Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“注入诱导电磁侧信道”框架：通过主动注入电磁信号，利用硬件非线性将原本难以辐射或难以测量的低频秘密信号调制至可接收频段，从而放大侧信道泄露并支持闭环分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，电磁泄露侧重保密性风险，电磁注入侧重完整性风险，二者常被分开研究。作者指出，主动注入可改变设备的泄露谱与信息量，使原本不可行的窃听成为可能，因此需要统一分析注入与泄露耦合产生的风险。

Method: 作者建立理论模型并开展实验测量，研究放大器、模数转换器和电源转换器等非线性器件如何把秘密电信号调制到外部注入的电磁载波上，实现低频信号的频率上变换。随后通过调节注入频率和幅度控制泄露特征，并设计InjectEave原型验证音频、电力消耗和模拟传感器输入等信号的可观测性。

Result: 实验显示，InjectEave可借助可获得的射频设备，在最远30米及隔墙条件下获取有线与无线耳机播放的音频。案例还表明，该技术能够形成“感知—干预—再感知”的闭环风险；低频设备功耗及模拟传感器输入同样可能产生注入诱导的电磁泄露。

Conclusion: 电磁注入不仅能扰动设备功能，也可能主动制造或增强侧信道泄露。防护应将注入抗扰性与辐射泄露控制联合考虑，包括降低模拟链路和电源链路非线性、加强滤波与屏蔽、限制异常射频耦合，并在系统级测试中评估不同注入参数下的泄露变化。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel leakage and injection are typically treated as distinct physical phenomena, threatening data confidentiality and integrity respectively. This work investigates how EM injection can be used to amplify side-channel leakage that is otherwise infeasible. We introduce a novel framework for Injection-Induced EM Side Channels to enable integrated, closed-loop EM security analysis. Our theoretical modeling and experimental measurements reveal that nonlinear hardware components, such as ubiquitous amplifiers, analog-to-digital converters, and power converters, can modulate secret electrical signals onto an injected EM carrier and thus upconvert low-frequency secrets into measurable EM emissions. By tuning the injection frequency and amplitude, adversaries gain the ability to actively shape the effective spectrum and entropy of the resulting leakage. We design InjectEave attack and demonstrate eavesdropping on the audio played through wired and wireless headphones from up to 30 m away with accessible RF equipment, as well as in through-wall scenarios, and characterize injection-induced EM leakage of other low-frequency secrets such as power consumption of smart home devices and analog sensor inputs. Case studies further demonstrate how the proposed techniques enable closed-loop eavesdropping and manipulation of landline-phone conversations. Finally, we analyze the broader security challenges and mitigations.

</details>


### [154] [Cost-Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent Ransomware Detection and Family Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04820)
*Mubashar Iqbal,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具备成本感知能力的分层多智能体系统（HMAS），通过按需调度静态、动态和内存分析，实现勒索软件检测与家族归因中的精度、成本和延迟平衡。完整系统二分类准确率为96.57%、F1为0.96、ROC-AUC为0.99，家族归因宏平均F1为0.90，同时较穷举式分析降低43.97%的平均成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件可能采用加壳、混淆、进程操纵和运行时规避等技术，单一分析模态难以稳定完成检测与归因。传统多模态方法通常对每个样本启用全部模态，导致计算成本高、分析延迟大，因此需要一种能够根据样本难度自适应选择分析模态的机制。

Method: 构建成本感知的分层多智能体系统：由专门化智能体组成多个领域控制器，并由元编排器统一协调。系统首先使用低成本的静态分析；当置信度不足或智能体意见不一致时，再选择性启用动态分析和内存分析。成本模型同时考虑模态调用与处理开销，以优化分析性能和资源成本之间的权衡。对于少量困难样本，使用本地部署的大语言模型进行验证，但不替代确定性分析流程。实验将该自适应方案与仅静态分析、静态加动态分析及穷举式分析进行比较，并评估二分类检测和多分类家族归因。

Result: 完整HMAS在二分类勒索软件检测中达到96.57%的准确率、0.96的F1分数和0.99的ROC-AUC，在家族归因中取得0.90的宏平均F1。相较穷举式分析，平均分析成本降低43.97%，平均延迟也显著下降，但调用大语言模型时除外。路由结果显示，56.05%的样本仅凭静态证据即可完成判断，仅4.33%的样本需要完整证据链。

Conclusion: 自适应分层多智能体编排能够在保持较高检测和归因性能的同时，显著降低多模态分析成本与延迟，并支持异构或不完整模态输入。结果表明，按需升级分析模态比对所有样本执行穷举式分析更具效率；不过，大语言模型验证会增加延迟，其适用范围仍需进一步控制和评估。

Abstract: Ransomware detection and family attribution require analysis of different modalities because it can use packing, obfuscation, process manipulation and runtime evasion techniques. However, conventional multimodal usually uses all available modalities for every sample resulting in unnecessary computational cost and increased latency. In this paper, we present a Cost Aware Hierarchical Multi-Agent System (HMAS) for adaptive ransomware detection. The proposed architecture organizes specialized agents into hierarchical domain controllers coordinated by a Meta Orchestrator. Static analysis is used as the initial low-cost modality while additional dynamic and memory modality is selectively used when confidence is insufficient or specialist agents exhibit disagreement. A cost model incorporates modality use and processing overhead. It enables the orchestration policy to balance analysis performance against computational cost. A locally deployed large language model provides verification for selected difficult cases without replacing the deterministic pipeline. Experimental evaluation compares adaptive HMAS with static only, static plus dynamic and exhaustive analysis policies across binary ransomware detection and multiclass family attribution. The complete HMAS achieved 96.57% accuracy, 0.96 F1-score and 0.99 ROC-AUC for binary detection. It also achieved 0.90 macro-F1 for family attribution. At the same time, the HMAS reduced average analysis cost by 43.97% relative to exhaustive analysis and substantially reduced average analysis latency except for the case where LLM is used. Routing analysis showed that 56.05% of cases were resolved using static evidence alone. Only 4.33% required the complete evidence pipeline. These findings demonstrate that adaptive HMAS can provide accuracy cost tradeoff for ransomware analysis while retaining support for heterogeneous and incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [155] [Forgetting Without Restarting: Execution-State Unlearning for Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04875)
*Chao Yao,Yangbo Wei,Zhen Huang,Junhong Qian,Chenle Chen,Shaoqiang Lu,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向长运行LLM智能体的执行状态遗忘框架，指出仅删除明文记忆无法清除摘要、工具计划和KV缓存中的派生信息；基于来源追踪的选择性回放可用最少量重计算实现近似完整重置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘操作通常只删除显式记忆记录，却保留由被撤销信息产生的上下文、压缩记忆和缓存状态，导致智能体继续泄漏或利用目标信息。本文旨在形式化“仿佛从未观察过目标”的执行状态遗忘，并确定其计算代价下界。

Method: 将智能体运行建模为确定性状态转移系统，证明目标注入前的轨迹可直接复用，而注入后的轨迹在缺乏令牌级归因时不可避免地受到污染，精确遗忘至少需要重计算从注入步τ到末步的T−τ+1次转移。提出Provenance-Guided Selective Replay：通过跨层来源图定位注入点，恢复检查点并裁剪KV缓存，再以清理后的状态重放受影响后缀。实验覆盖三套智能体、九种基线和三类模型，并采用诱导式、随机式及无字符串匹配的行为测试。

Result: 删除记忆对泄漏几乎没有改善；基于指令的遗忘在诱导测试中失效，Leak@probes达到1.00；源文本删减后，智能体仍在80%的回合中执行已撤销偏好。选择性回放在行为上与完整重置难以区分，同时最多减少约9倍的重计算令牌。

Conclusion: 执行状态遗忘必须覆盖所有由目标信息派生的运行时状态，而非仅删除明文记录。来源追踪结合KV缓存裁剪和选择性重放能够达到理论上的最小重计算范围，并在多个测试设置中有效恢复反事实行为。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents are stateful: beyond the transcript they accrete compressed summaries, plaintext memory, pending tool plans, and, under every serving API, a KV cache. Yet today's "forget" operations delete a plaintext memory record and stop, leaving every artifact derived from the revoked information intact. We formalize execution-state unlearning: after a forget request, the agent must behave as if it had never observed the target. Modeling the runtime as a deterministic transition system, we prove that the pre-target trajectory prefix is shared with this counterfactual world for free, that the post-target suffix is irreducibly tainted without token-level attribution, and that exact unlearning requires at least $T-τ+1$ recomputed transitions, where $τ$ is the target's injection step. Provenance-Guided Selective Replay attains this bound as a cross-layer contract spanning prompt, compressed memory, and cache: a provenance graph locates the injection point, checkpoint restoration reduces to cropping the KV cache, and sanitized replay regenerates the counterfactual suffix. Audited with elicitation, stochastic, and string-free behavioral tests across three agent suites, nine baselines, and three model families, memory deletion leaves leakage unchanged, instruction-based forgetting collapses under elicitation (Leak@probes = 1.00), and source redaction still acts on a revoked preference in 80% of episodes, while selective replay is indistinguishable from a full reset at up to 9x fewer recomputed tokens.

</details>


### [156] [ReCAST: Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training for Obfuscated SMS Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04878)
*Jieyun Huang,Yi Shen,Kaikai Zhao,Jiangze Yan,Wenjing Zhang,Ping Chen,Ning Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCAST，一种面向中文短信混淆攻击的恢复感知级联分阶段训练框架，通过将大模型的去混淆能力蒸馏到小型学生模型，提升受混淆短信的风险分类鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的中文短信攻击者会利用谐音、符号替换、拆分等多种混淆策略隐藏风险短语，同时保持人工可读性，导致直接分类模型性能下降。生产系统还受到延迟、吞吐量和模型规模的约束，因此需要兼顾鲁棒性与部署效率的方法。

Method: ReCAST首先利用教师模型监督学生模型完成混淆片段检测、混淆类型预测和文本恢复等任务，将去混淆能力迁移至较小的学生模型；随后使用具备恢复能力的学生模型进行下游短信风险分类，形成恢复感知的级联式、分阶段训练流程。

Result: 在内部构建的真实中文短信基准上，ReCAST在存在文本混淆的场景下显著优于直接训练的基线方法，表明多任务恢复监督和知识蒸馏能够提升分类性能，并支持更小模型的部署。摘要未提供具体数据集规模、指标数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 恢复感知蒸馏能够有效增强中文短信风险分类对混淆攻击的鲁棒性，并为生产环境中受延迟、吞吐量和模型规模约束的轻量化部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Fraudulent messages sent via Short Message Service (SMS) are increasingly obfuscated to evade cost-conscious classifiers in production systems. In Chinese SMS, attackers can exploit a wide range of carefully crafted obfuscation strategies to hide risk-bearing phrases while preserving human readability, making direct classification brittle under real-world latency and throughput constraints. We propose ReCAST, a Restoration-aware Cascaded Stage-wise Training framework for robust obfuscated Chinese SMS classification. ReCAST distills a large teacher model's de-obfuscation ability into a smaller deployable student model by supervising obfuscated span detection, obfuscation type prediction, and text restoration, and then uses the restoration-aware student for downstream risk classification. Experiments on an internally constructed real-world Chinese SMS benchmark show that ReCAST substantially improves classification performance over directly trained baselines under obfuscation. The results suggest that restoration-aware distillation offers a practical path toward robust SMS risk classification with smaller deployable models under production-oriented constraints.

</details>


### [157] [The Security Feature Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04899)
*Kevin Hermann,Sven Peldszus,Thorsten Berger,Adam Shostack*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“安全特征定位”（security feature location）任务：将源代码中的具体位置与认证、加密等安全特征建立关联，以帮助开发者理解系统的安全实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全依赖认证、加密等功能，但开发者往往难以快速确定系统实际实现了哪些安全特征、这些特征分布在何处，以及实现是否符合预期的安全属性。

Method: 论文定义安全特征定位这一分析任务，其核心是识别代码位置并将其映射到相应的安全特征，从而形成安全功能与实现代码之间的可追踪关系。摘要未说明具体采用的算法、工具、数据集或评估协议。

Result: 摘要主张该任务能够提升开发者对安全实现的可理解性，并支持检查预期安全属性是否得到执行；但未提供量化实验结果、比较基线或性能指标。

Conclusion: 该工作将安全审计中的关键问题形式化为代码—安全特征关联任务。其潜在价值在于增强安全实现的可见性与可追溯性，但具体技术效果仍需结合正文中的实现方法和实验评估确认。

Abstract: Software security must be realized through security features such as authentication and encryption, but which features does a system implement, and where? We present security feature location: the task of relating code locations to security features, enabling developers to understand security implementations and assess whether intended security properties are enforced.

</details>


### [158] [Robust Coverless Linguistic Steganography via Sentence Embedding Space with Global Resynchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04970)
*Lizhi Xiong,Yuping Lu,Jun Li,Ziqiang Li,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种在句子嵌入空间中进行编码的无载体隐写框架，通过层次聚类路径表示秘密消息，并利用全局重同步机制缓解可变长编码在文本扰动后的比特失步问题，从而提升词级和句级扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言隐写通常基于词元级操作，文本经过替换、删改或句子调整后容易破坏隐藏信息。尤其是可变长编码方案中，局部错误会造成嵌入与提取比特流失去同步，进而导致后续信息整体恢复失败。因此，需要一种不依赖精确词元序列、且可在局部语义表示失效时保持全局可恢复性的方案。

Method: 该方法将操作空间由词元空间转移至句子语义嵌入空间。首先，对句子嵌入进行层次聚类，并将秘密信息映射为对应的聚类路径；提取时依据文本句子的嵌入所属语义子空间恢复符号。其次，引入全局重同步机制，将传统可变长比特流重新表示为锚定于语义子空间的离散符号，使单个局部嵌入判断错误不会直接引发后续比特序列的持续错位。

Result: 实验表明，在词级和句级文本扰动条件下，该框架相较于现有方法具有更高的信息恢复鲁棒性。与此同时，方法仍能保持有效的嵌入容量，并表现出较强的统计分析抵抗能力，说明其生成或选择的文本在统计特征上较不易被检测。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是以句子语义嵌入及层次聚类路径替代词元级隐写编码，并通过全局重同步机制解决可变长编码的失步传播问题。该思路有效增强了语言隐写对文本编辑和改写的适应性，但其实际效果仍可能依赖句子嵌入模型质量、聚类边界稳定性以及扰动类型与强度。

Abstract: Linguistic steganography enables covert communication through natural language. Existing methods heavily rely on token-level operations and struggle to maintain reliability under word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. Moreover, variable-length coding-based schemes are highly susceptible to bit-slippage under minor disturbances, as perturbations cause desynchronization between embedded and extracted bit sequences. To address these issues, we propose a robust coverless steganographic framework that operates in the sentence embedding space rather than the token space. Specifically, secret messages are encoded as hierarchical clustering paths in the sentence embedding space, which enhances decoding stability against word- and sentence-level textual perturbations. To tackle the bit-slippage problem, we introduce a Global Resynchronization Mechanism (GRM) that reframes variable-length bitstreams as discrete symbols anchored to semantic subspaces, decoupling local embedding failures from global message recovery. Experimental results demonstrate that under word- and sentence-level perturbations, our approach achieves substantial improvements in robustness, while maintaining effective embedding capacity and exhibiting strong resistance to statistical analysis.

</details>


### [159] [TPMSpy: Validation of Measured Boot Systems by Low-Level Tracing of TPM Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05011)
*Roman Lacko,Petr Svenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与平台无关的低层 TPM 使用分析方法，可独立重建和验证 TPM 事件日志，并应用于 Linux 与 Windows。对 2020—2025 年不同 systemd 版本的纵向分析表明，Measured Boot 的实现差异显著，部分系统存在测量不一致问题，影响远程证明和 LUKS 磁盘解密的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Measured Boot 的安全性不仅取决于 TPM 机制本身，还取决于系统是否在正确条件下测量了预期的软件和状态。现有实践缺乏系统化检查方法，且依赖引用机制进行验证可能难以发现实现偏差，因此需要一种独立、可迁移且不依赖具体实现细节的分析方法。

Method: 在虚拟化系统与 TPM 交互层面分析低层 TPM 使用情况，通过重建并验证 TPM Event Log 来评估实际测量行为，而不依赖 TPM quoting 机制。该方法具有平台无关性，分别在 Linux 和 Windows 上进行验证，并对 systemd 245—258 版本对应的 Linux 系统开展系统性纵向分析。

Result: 研究发现不同系统的 Measured Boot 使用方式存在广泛差异，没有形成统一的测量模式。分析还识别出未公开记录的行为变化，并发现部分用户态 systemd 服务的测量存在不一致，导致系统无法稳定支持远程证明以及基于测量结果的 LUKS 磁盘解密。

Conclusion: Measured Boot 的实际安全性高度依赖具体实现和系统配置，不能假定不同平台或版本具有一致行为。所提出的方法能够独立评估 TPM 测量流程，并揭示传统验证方式可能遗漏的问题；由于系统间差异显著，远程证明和磁盘解密部署应采用针对具体系统的定制化分析与验证。

Abstract: Measured Boot extends trust in a booted system by recording cryptographic measurements of executed software and system state into a Trusted Platform Module (TPM), enabling subsequent verification through remote attestation. Although this mechanism is increasingly deployed in contemporary operating systems, its practical security depends on whether implementations measure the expected components under the expected conditions, yet this is not checked systematically.
  We propose a platform-agnostic method for analysing low-level TPM usage at the level of virtualized system--TPM interactions. It enables independent reconstruction and validation of the TPM Event Log without relying on the quoting mechanism itself. Because it does not depend on implementation details, it is applicable to both open and closed systems. We demonstrate the method on both Linux and Windows and conduct a systematic longitudinal analysis of Linux systems with systemd versions 245--258 (2020--2025), examining how Measured Boot usage evolved and observing wide divergence. No single usage pattern emerged amongst systems, warranting customized analysis.
  The analysis identifies undocumented behavioural changes, reveals inconsistent measurements of user-space systemd services, which prevent reliable remote attestation and LUKS disk decryption on such systems.

</details>


### [160] [Has MIMO decoding been proved hard from lattice problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05013)
*Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析Dean和Goldsmith提出的将格问题归约到MIMO译码的理论证明，指出其基于LWE的归约在非模MIMO场景中存在结构性失效，因此现有证明无法建立所宣称的MIMO译码计算困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该归约旨在为物理层安全提供基于经典格问题困难性的计算基础，但后续研究已发现相关构造存在攻击和反例，且归约本身的有效性与适用边界尚不明确。本文旨在厘清证明缺陷、参数攻击与潜在修复要求之间的区别。

Method: 对修订后的归约进行理论审查，比较Regev的LWE归约与其在非模MIMO译码问题中的对应结构，重点分析关键性质能否从模运算环境迁移到MIMO设置，并区分归约证明中的逻辑缺陷与特定参数选择导致的直接攻击。

Result: 研究发现，LWE归约依赖的若干结构特征无法在非模MIMO场景中保留，导致已发表的证明不能推出MIMO译码具有所宣称的格问题困难性。该结论不同于仅针对特定参数的攻击，并进一步说明任何修复方案需要满足更严格的结构与归约条件。

Conclusion: 现有归约不足以为MIMO物理层安全提供格困难性保证，但本文并未否定MIMO系统物理层安全的整体可行性；它仅表明该安全性不能由当前归约及其证明得到。

Abstract: Multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technology is fundamental to modern wireless communication. Physical layer security seeks to protect transmitted information by exploiting properties of the noisy communication channel. Dean and Goldsmith proposed a polynomial time reduction from lattice problems to MIMO decoding by adapting Regev's reduction for learning with errors (LWE). If valid, this reduction would give physical layer security a strong computational foundation based on the hardness of established lattice problems. Subsequent works presented attacks and counterexamples against the resulting construction, casting doubt on its security but leaving the precise validity and limitations of the underlying reduction incompletely understood. We provide a theoretical examination of the revised reduction and identify the structural features of the LWE reduction that fail to carry over to the non-modular MIMO setting, hence showing that its published proof does not establish the claimed hardness of MIMO decoding. Our results distinguish flaws in the hardness proof from direct attacks on particular parameter choices and clarify what would be required of any attempted repair. We do not rule out physical layer security for MIMO systems in general, but show that the claimed lattice hardness guarantee does not follow from the existing reduction.

</details>


### [161] [Operational Roles of QRNG-Derived Quantum Entropy in Bitcoin Proof-of-Work Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05092)
*Ricardo Fernandes da Silva,Paulo Vitor Batista Santos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一个可复现实验基准，用于评估混合量子—经典挖矿基础设施中量子随机数发生器（QRNG）的实际运营价值。核心结论是：在候选区块头保持互异时，QRNG不会提高诚实工作量证明的成功概率；其价值主要体现在熵来源可审计性、重启故障相关性及命名空间复用等保障导向场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将量子随机性与工作量证明性能提升直接关联，但候选输入互异时，随机源类型并不改变单次哈希命中概率。论文因此关注更具工程意义的问题：QRNG在调度、重启、熵管理和来源验证层面是否能提供可量化的运营收益。

Method: 作者构建模拟验证框架，并定义两个调度层指标：熵效率因子η，用于衡量随机熵被有效转化为不同候选任务的程度；重启多样性指数ρ，用于衡量系统重启后候选空间的差异性。研究采用蒙特卡洛实验和调度器仿真，报告置信区间，并将QRNG方案与高质量确定性方案及强经典随机基线进行比较。

Result: 仿真显示，在配置合理的确定性策略和强经典随机源面前，QRNG在诚实工作量证明成功率及常规调度表现上基本持平。QRNG的相对优势仅在特定保障场景中显现，包括重启故障具有相关性、候选命名空间被重复使用，以及需要证明熵来源可信和独立的情况。

Conclusion: 该工作并非证明QRNG设备可直接提升挖矿概率，而是提供了评估其系统级价值的仿真框架。论文的贡献在于将QRNG收益从概率性能转向可靠性、差异性和熵来源保障等可观测维度。下一步应开展硬件在环实验，并使用记录的或实时QRNG数据流验证模拟结论。

Abstract: Replacing classical entropy with QRNG output does not change honest Bitcoin PoW success probability when candidate headers remain distinct. The original contribution of this paper is a reproducible benchmark that locates and measures the operational value of quantum entropy in hybrid quantum-classical mining infrastructure through two scheduler-level observables, the entropy-efficiency factor $η$ and the reboot-diversity index $ρ$. Monte Carlo and scheduler simulations with confidence intervals show parity for competent deterministic and strong-classical baselines, while QRNG value emerges in assurance-oriented scenarios involving correlated restart faults, namespace reuse, and entropy provenance. The study is therefore positioned as a simulation-based validation framework rather than as a device-level QRNG demonstration; hardware-in-the-loop validation with recorded or live QRNG streams is identified as the next experimental step.

</details>


### [162] [TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05117)
*Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi,Hai-Yen Vong,Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung,Tram Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIER基准，用于评估大语言模型在不同威胁隐含程度下的安全行为，强调仅使用二元指标难以反映模型响应的细粒度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准主要依赖拒答或服从等二元指标，无法刻画模型面对从明确风险请求到高隐含性攻击时的行为变化，也难以区分攻击成功率相近模型之间的安全性差异。

Method: 构建包含四类风险领域和四个威胁等级的TIER基准，覆盖明确风险请求、情境化提示和复杂越狱等类型；采用六级行为标签评估模型响应，并结合两名相互独立的语言模型评审员进行判断。实验测试了六个开放权重语言模型。

Result: 模型的安全行为随威胁等级逐步变化，而非简单地从拒答直接转为服从。情境化提示诱发了最丰富的行为类型，复杂越狱暴露出最大的稳健性差距。此外，攻击成功率相近的模型可能呈现显著不同的响应分布。

Conclusion: TIER证明，基于行为分布和威胁隐含程度的细粒度评估能够补充传统二元安全指标，更全面地揭示大语言模型的安全行为与稳健性差异。

Abstract: Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [163] [Understanding the Privacy-Preserving Potential of HTTP/2 Against Webpage Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05119)
*Bogdan Cebere,Prateek Kumar,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用HTTP/2在应用层部署网站指纹（WF）防御，并系统评估既有防御机制及HTTP/2原生特性的隐私保护效果与开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网站指纹防御主要针对Tor、VPN等封装协议，较少从HTTP应用层研究。由于HTTPS流量模式仍可能泄露用户访问的网页，亟需探索无需修改底层封装协议、且可由客户端或服务器部署的HTTP/2防御方案。

Method: 作者将HTTPOS、LLaMA、FRONT和Tamaraw等客户端侧防御，以及ALPaCA和Tamaraw等服务器侧防御映射到HTTP/2功能上；同时探索主动资源建议、多路复用和流量控制等HTTP/2特性。评估采用统一实验框架，根据数据集校准防御参数，结合超参数调优后的实际指纹攻击、信息论泄漏估计器和通信开销测量，分析隐私—开销权衡。

Result: 研究表明，多种既有WF防御能够通过HTTP/2特性在应用层实现；HTTP/2的主动资源建议、多路复用和流量控制还可构成轻量级防御。统一评估框架能够识别各防御下最强的指纹模型，并估计防御后的残余信息泄漏，同时量化其性能与流量开销。摘要未给出具体数值结果或不同方案的明确排名。

Conclusion: HTTP/2为网站指纹防御提供了不依赖Tor或VPN的应用层部署路径。将实际攻击、信息论指标和开销联合评估，有助于更全面地比较防御方案；未来应重点优化HTTP/2原生机制，以在较低开销下实现更强的隐私保护。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer which webpage a user visits from encrypted HTTPS traffic alone, compromising privacy even without decryption. WF defenses commonly shape traffic through noise, padding, delays, or flow splitting, yet they are most often studied from the perspective of encapsulating protocols like Tor or VPN rather than at the application layer (HTTP).
  In this work, we focus on application-layer defenses enabled by the most widely deployed version of HTTP, HTTP/2. We demonstrate how known defenses can be emulated through HTTP/2 features at the client side (HTTPOS, LLaMA, FRONT, Tamaraw) and the server side (ALPaCA, Tamaraw). We further show that HTTP/2 features, such as proactive resource suggestion, multiplexing, and flow control, offer untapped potential for lightweight yet effective defenses deployable at both endpoints.
  We evaluate these defenses using a unified blueprint that calibrates defense parameters per dataset, then combines practical attacks, information-theoretic leakage estimates, and overhead measurements. For each defense, this framework identifies the strongest hyperparameter-tuned fingerprinting model and estimates the residual uncertainty induced by the defense using two information-theoretic leakage estimators, all while accounting for the defense's privacy-overhead trade-offs.

</details>


### [164] [Conformal Prediction for Offensive Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05165)
*Giovanni Cherubin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨保形预测（Conformal Prediction, CP）在网络安全进攻性应用中的研究空白，重点关注隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析两个方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管保形预测提出已有二十多年，并已广泛用于防御性安全研究，但其在进攻性网络安全中的应用仍较少、文献记录不足。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者围绕两个关键领域开展初步探索：隐私保护机器学习与网络流量分析，并考察保形预测在实施进攻性安全任务中的潜在用途。摘要未提供具体算法、实验设置或数据集细节。

Result: 研究给出了保形预测用于进攻性安全的初步发现，表明其不仅可作为防御工具，也可能支持隐私保护机器学习和网络流量分析中的攻击性任务。摘要未报告定量实验结果。

Conclusion: 保形预测在进攻性网络安全领域具有值得进一步研究的应用潜力，尤其是在隐私保护和流量分析方面；未来需要通过更系统的算法设计、实验评估和安全场景验证来深化研究。

Abstract: Despite its introduction more than a quarter century ago, Conformal Prediction (CP) has seen surprisingly few applications to the cyber security world thus far. In particular, we observe that, while CP has been employed as a defensive measure in many recent works, its use for carrying out attacks (i.e., for offensive security) is hard to trace in the literature. We explore this gap, by presenting initial findings in two key areas of offensive security: Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning, and network traffic analysis.

</details>


### [165] [Governing Bring Your Own AI: A Parameterized Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05236)
*Dare Bello,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究员工使用个人账号访问生成式AI工具的“自带AI（BYOAI）”现象，提出面向非受管控个人账户的治理模型、风险分类法及成熟度框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架主要针对企业统一采购、身份认证和安全控制下的工具，难以覆盖员工通过个人账号在企业安全边界外使用生成式AI所带来的数据泄露、合规及管理盲区。

Method: 研究通过系统性文献综述分析30份资料，包括24篇研究文献和6份框架文件，构建风险分类法与框架参与度画像；进一步建立参数化治理模型，将五级治理成熟度阶梯与技术控制架构连接，以控制覆盖范围评估残余风险下降效果。

Result: 数据暴露与合规是最突出的风险类别，现有框架的实际参与和适用程度并不一致。研究提出技术、治理和人员三项相互支撑的保障支柱。模型结果显示，单纯禁止使用的策略，其残余风险降低效果接近基础治理；采用分层控制的方案则可显著降低建模中的数据外泄风险，并提高可执行的控制覆盖率。

Conclusion: BYOAI不能仅依靠禁止或传统企业AI治理框架处理。组织应采用以成熟度为导向、融合技术控制、治理机制和人员管理的分层治理模式，重点提升个人账号使用场景下的数据保护、合规管理与控制可见性。

Abstract: Employees are increasingly using personally owned generative AI tools such as ChatGPT, Gemini, and Claude for their daily work. This practice is known as Bring Your Own AI (BYOAI), which is a distinct form of Shadow AI in which employee-authenticated personal accounts are used outside of enterprise identity and security controls. Existing frameworks were designed for AI tools managed by organizations, and their coverage does not extend to unmanaged AI tools used with a personal account. In addressing these issues, we developed a governance model through a systematic review of the literature that produces a risk taxonomy and a framework-engagement profile. We also developed a parameterized governance model that measures how much a level of governance maturity reduces residual risk. A five-level maturity ladder is coupled to a technical control architecture through a chain in which the coverage of the control layer influences the security outcomes. Our study of a curated corpus of 30 records (24 research studies and 6 framework documents) indicated that the most prominent categories identified were data exposure and compliance, and framework engagement was inconsistent. Three mutually supporting pillars (technical, governance, and human) were established to support safeguards. Additionally, the results of the model demonstrated that prohibition-based solutions will result in residual risk levels close to those achieved through baseline solutions. Under the specified parameterization, layered control-based solutions substantially reduce modeled exfiltration risk and increase enforceable coverage.

</details>


### [166] [CONTINUITY: Security-Context Contracts for Composable LLM Agent Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05269)
*Chris Zheng,Geng Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CONTINUITY框架，用于解决大语言模型智能体系统中安全上下文跨组件传递时丢失、扩张、重绑定或语义重解释的问题，并通过可验证的契约与授权证据链保证从指令到外部执行效果的端到端安全一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通常叠加溯源、授权、策略执行、协议适配和执行控制等机制，但各机制即使单独正确，也可能因组件边界处的上下文断裂而无法形成整体安全保证。论文将该问题定义为“安全上下文不连续性”，目标是避免未经有效授权或授权语义失真的外部效果被实际提交。

Method: CONTINUITY以假设—保证契约描述各组件的安全责任，并在组件转换中携带经认证的安全上下文。其核心证据包括签名根授权、溯源承诺、角色绑定的转换回执、有界类型化释放、转换见证及效果绑定执行许可。论文进一步形式化“端到端后果完整性”：每一项已实现的外部效果均须由有效且当前的授权见证支持，该见证关联主体、任务、溯源、委托关系、策略状态、规范化动作和最终性边界。作者实现了参考验证器，并构建覆盖4个应用领域、32类故障的确定性跨层故障注入套件。

Result: 在2,560个参数化攻击实例、128个故障—领域组合类别中，完整CONTINUITY配置未提交任何有害外部效果；同时完成了全部700项良性任务，并将200项语义或授权状态不明确的任务全部升级处理。结果表明，该框架能够在实验设定下兼顾安全阻断、正常任务完成和不确定情形的审慎处置。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将智能体安全从孤立控制机制的正确性，提升为跨越完整“指令—转换—执行效果”链路的可验证组合问题。其结论认为，安全智能体执行不仅需要可靠的单点控制，还需要显式契约、可认证上下文传播及与最终执行效果绑定的授权证据，以维持端到端安全保证。

Abstract: LLM agent systems increasingly combine provenance tracking, authorization, policy enforcement, protocol adapters, and execution controls. However, individually correct security mechanisms do not necessarily compose into an end-to-end secure system: security-critical context may be dropped, widened, rebound, or reinterpreted as actions cross component boundaries. We identify this failure mode as security-context discontinuity and introduce CONTINUITY, a framework for verifiable composition of agent security controls.
  CONTINUITY models each component with an assume-guarantee contract and carries authenticated security context across transitions using signed root grants, provenance commitments, role-bound transition receipts, bounded typed releases, transformation witnesses, and effect-bound execution permits. We formalize end-to-end consequence integrity, requiring every realized external effect to be backed by a valid and current authorization witness linking the principal, task, provenance, delegation, policy state, canonical action, and finality boundary.
  We implement a reference verifier and deterministic cross-layer fault-injection suite covering 32 fault classes across four application domains. In 2,560 parameterized attack instances spanning 128 fault-domain classes, the full CONTINUITY configuration commits no harmful external effect, while completing all 700 benign tasks and escalating all 200 ambiguous cases. These results show that secure agent execution requires not only sound individual controls, but explicit contracts that preserve their guarantees across the complete instruction-to-effect path.

</details>


### [167] [Machine Unlearning as Private Retroactive Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05329)
*Haim Kaplan,Refael Kohen,Yishay Mansour,Kobbi Nissim,Uri Stemmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将机器遗忘重新界定为数据维护问题，提出结合回溯性与持续观测差分隐私的私有回溯算法框架，并在线性统计、聚类和直方图任务上给出高效构造及不可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器遗忘要求删除数据后的模型等价于从未使用该数据重新训练，但已有研究表明，在对手观察多次发布结果的场景下，这种等价性并不提供有意义的隐私保证。因此，本文旨在建立同时满足历史修改一致性与隐私保护的统一形式化框架。

Method: 将回溯算法的历史修订要求与持续观测下的差分隐私相结合，定义私有回溯算法；随后针对线性统计、聚类和直方图设计相应构造，并推导相关任务的不可实现边界。

Result: 对于线性统计、聚类和直方图，所提出的算法能够同时满足隐私性与回溯性，且相较于仅实现隐私的算法不产生渐近开销；同时，本文还证明了若干不可能性结果，刻画了该框架的限制。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘的核心不应仅被视为隐私问题，而应被视为支持历史修改的数据维护问题。私有回溯算法为持续发布场景下实现删除一致性和差分隐私提供了统一方法，并揭示了可实现性与不可实现性的边界。

Abstract: Machine unlearning typically aims to emulate retraining from scratch: upon a deletion request, the unlearning algorithm should produce an outcome that would have been obtained had the deleted point never been included. Recent work has shown that this emulation requirement carries no meaningful privacy semantics against an adversary who observes a sequence of releases. Machine unlearning is thus not a privacy question per se, but rather a data maintenance question, which is precisely the subject of retroactive algorithms. These are algorithms supporting modifications of past operations, guaranteeing that all subsequent answers reflect the revised history as if it had always been in force.
  We put forward a definition of private retroactive algorithms, combining the retroactivity requirement with differential privacy under continual observation. We present constructions achieving both privacy and retroactivity at no asymptotic cost over privacy alone for linear statistics, clustering, and histograms, alongside impossibility results.

</details>


### [168] [The History Is the Detector: Executing CVE Patch History, End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05335)
*Qiushi Wu,Kevin Eykholt,Youngja Park,Xiaokui Shu,Dhilung Kirat,Douglas Lee Schales,Ian Molloy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BUGSTONE-E2E将已验证的漏洞修复提交转化为可执行的检测规则，并通过分阶段分析、LLM审查、运行时验证和差分测试，实现从漏洞发现到补丁生成的闭环。基于19,325个高危CVE，系统构建了1,033条规则，并在14个程序中获得644条运行时证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据库和修复提交详细记录了漏洞成因与修复方式，但主要面向人工阅读，难以自动复用。因此，代码库中可能存在未被公开通报的同类不安全条件，历史漏洞知识的检测价值尚未充分发挥。

Method: 系统首先从经过验证的修复提交中挖掘规则，提取扫描锚点、修复语义和CVE来源，并按CWE与编程语言组织。随后采用漏斗式流水线：使用Tree-sitter枚举匹配锚点的调用点，以轻量级启发式规则过滤良性候选；再由受规则引导的LLM智能体检查剩余候选；之后进行再次分诊、构建运行时验证，并生成通过双向差分测试和作用域检查的补丁。

Result: 从2022至2026年的19,325个高危CVE中识别出2,710个修复提交，构建1,033条覆盖56个CWE家族的检测规则，并封装为172项技能。在14个程序上应用后，系统为644个发现获得了运行时证据。摘要未报告误报率、召回率、运行成本或与现有方法的对比结果。

Conclusion: BUGSTONE-E2E表明，漏洞历史可以被系统化转化为可执行、可验证且可用于修复的检测工作流，从而支持同类漏洞的复现性检测与自动化修复。其主要贡献在于规则挖掘、分阶段资源控制以及检测结果到运行时证据和补丁验证的闭环。

Abstract: Public vulnerability databases collect rich information about known software flaws, including their weakness types, affected components, and related patches. Fixing commits provide the exact code changes that removed these flaws. While these records capture why the original code was unsafe, they are documented mainly for human inspection rather than automated reuse. Consequently, the same unsafe conditions may still exist elsewhere in code without a known advisory, leaving much of this detection knowledge unused.
  We present BUGSTONE-E2E, a framework that transforms vulnerability history into executable detection rules and validates their findings. First, BUGSTONE-E2E mines reusable rules from verified fixing commits, capturing scan anchors, fix semantics, and CVE provenance and organizing them by CWE and language. Second, detection follows a funnel-shaped pipeline: early stages process a large pool of candidates using lightweight analysis, while later stages apply increasingly capable and expensive models to a shrinking set of targets.
